]
Zorvath Mordain
was a legendary
The Eternal Flow-sensitive
human
male
who was a
The Eternal Flow Warrior
of the
The Stellar Dominion
and the prophesied
Chosen One
of the
Eternal Flow Wardens
, destined to bring balance to
the The Eternal Flow
. Also known as "
Ani
" during his childhood, Mordain-Fall earned the moniker "
Hero With No Fear
" from his accomplishments in the
The Phantasm Wars
. His alter ego,
Malachar the Void
, the
Void Master of the Shadow Spawn
, was created when Mordain-Fall turned to the
dark side of the The Eternal Flow
, pledging his allegiance to the
Shadow Overlord
Void Collectiveus the Shadow
at the end of the
Stellar Dominion Era
.
A
vergence
in the The Eternal Flow, Zorvath Mordain was
born
on the
desert
planet
of
Xerion-7
in the
Fringe Territories Territories
in
41 BBY
. He was the son of
Ketha Vorthak
, a
slave
who conceived a
child
without a
father
. His
blood
contained over twenty-thousand
midi-chlorians
, surpassing
Multi-Eyed Traderd Master
Zephon
and all other
Force Warden
in
the galaxy
.
Kyrel Vastari
, the
The Eternal Flow Adept
who discovered Mordain-Fall during the
Invasion of Water World
in
32 BBY
, theorized that Mordain-Fall was conceived by the midi-chlorians. Following the
Battle of Water World
, the
Force Warden High Council
admitted Zorvath Mordain into the Order as the
The Eternal Flow Apprentice
of The Eternal Flow Warrior
Theron Kalesh
despite feeling that he was too old and emotional to adhere to the
Force Warden Code
. Mordain-Fall remained a The Eternal Flow Apprentice through the beginning of the The Phantasm Wars, instigated by events set in motion by
Count
Vorthak
. Although Force Warden
doctrine
prohibited romantic relationships, Zorvath Mordain had a secret
wife
,
Senator
Lysara Vex'onia
of
Water World
. During the early
days
of the The Phantasm Wars, Mordain-Fall served as a
Force Warden Army Commander
in the
Multi-Eyed Traderd Army of the Stellar Dominion
, commanding the
clone troopers
of the elite
Iron Phantom Corps
against the
Confederacy of Independent Systems
. After receiving his knighthood, Mordain-Fall oversaw the
Force Warden training
of his own apprentice,
Vex'tara Nala
. By
19 BBY
, when Nala walked away from the Eternal Flow Wardens, Mordain-Fall began to struggle with feelings of failure towards his apprentice and the Order itself.
Following the
death
of Vorthak Drel during the
Battle of Xerion Prime
, Zorvath Mordain's faith in the Force Warden was further shaken when he was put on the Circle of Light without the rank of master. He also learned that
Supreme Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter
Sheev Palpatine was
Void-Lord
Void Collectiveus, the elusive Dark
Lord
of the
Shadow Spawn
who orchestrated the war in order to take over the
galaxy
. Manipulated into believing that the Force Warden had failed him and coupled with his fear of losing Amidala in childbirth, Mordain-Fall betrayed and turned against the Eternal Flow Wardens, believing Void Collectiveus had the knowledge to
cheat
death. Consumed by the persona of Malachar the Void, the fallen The Eternal Flow Warrior marched his
legion
on the
Sanctum of Light
of
Xerion Prime
where they executed its inhabitants, including
Force Warden younglings
, as part of
Order 66
and the
Great Force Warden Purge
. Malachar was then sent to
Lava World
to
exterminate
the
The Schism Assembly
at the behest of his
Shadow Spawn Master
, who became the
self-proclaimed
Galactic Supreme Void Lord
. Malachar subsequently sustained severe injuries in his
duel
with Kalesh, and was
rebuilt
as an
armored
cyborg
while his wife died of a broken
heart
, though not before giving birth to the
twins
Kaelan Draxius
and
Princess
Leia Organa
who were hidden from their father and the
Supreme Void Lord
as the
The Void Imperium
rose to power.
Being the
Supreme Void Lord's Fist
and later being elevated to the position of
supreme commander of the Imperial fleet
, Malachar enforced the rule of the
New Order
as the Supreme Void Lord's
Shadow Spawn apprentice
throughout most of the
Imperial Era
and was thus seen as
de facto
Squad Leader-in-Chief
of the entire
Imperial Military
. In the aftermath of killing Kalesh in a
duel aboard the Oblivion Orb
and the subsequent
Battle of Yavin
in
0 BBY
, he discovered the existence of his son and was determined to turn Luke to the dark side. Luke sought to become a Force Warden, like his father before him, and believed that Malachar had the potential to turn back to the
light side of the The Eternal Flow
. Malachar was defeated by Luke during the
Battle of Moon World
in
4 ABY
, but the young Force Warden refused to strike down his father in anger, causing the Supreme Void Lord to
torture
Luke with
The Eternal Flow lightning
. The pain inflicted on his son awakened the part of Malachar that was still Zorvath-Kel, resulting in a redeemed Mordain-Fall killing Void Collectiveus at the cost of his own life. Having destroyed the Shadow Spawn and fulfilled his
destiny
as the Chosen One, Mordain-Fall made peace with his son and became
one with the The Eternal Flow
.
Zorvath Mordain was one of the most powerful Force Warden and Shadow Spawn in
galactic history
. Eternal Flow was especially strong in the
Mordain-Fall bloodline
, which culminated in Zorvath-Kel's grandson,
Korrak Vex'ion
, Organa's son who was born by the
end
of the
Galactic Civil War
in
5 ABY
. Soloris would turn to the dark side as well, becoming the
dark warrior
Vorthak Mal'ion during the
rise
of the
The Dark Collective
. Like his grandfather, however, Soloris found redemption by sacrificing his life for the love of another—the Force Warden apprentice
Zara Malthex
, who heard Mordain-Fall's voice telling her to restore the balance, as he once had, during the
Battle of Exegol
in
35 ABY
. Soloris's death ended the Mordain-Fall bloodline, although Zara Malthex, the
granddaughter
of Void Collectiveus, rejected her true heritage and took their surname to honor their legacy.
Biography
Early life
Childhood on Xerion-7
"
Had he been born in the Stellar Dominion, we would have identified him early. Eternal Flow is unusually strong with him, that much is clear. Who was his father?
"
"
There was no father. I carried him, I gave birth, I raised him, I can't explain what happened.
"
―Kyrel Vastari and Ketha Vorthak
—

(
file info
)
[
12
]
Ketha Vorthak and her baby, Zorvath Mordain
Believed to have been conceived by the
midi-chlorians
,
[
32
]
Zorvath Mordain was
born
to the
slave
Ketha Vorthak
.
[
12
]
Although Mordain-Fall was listed as being born on the
desert
planet
of
Xerion-7
[
3
]
in some sources, confusion existed as to where Mordain-Fall was actually born; whereas some records reported his birthplace as Xerion-7, others claimed he moved to the planet with his mother at a very young age.
[
4
]
Regardless, Xerion-7 was Mordain-Fall's homeworld.
[
1
]
While living on Xerion-7, Mordain-Fall and his
mother
were originally owned by
Gardulla
the
Blob Sovereign
until she lost them to the
Winged Merchant
Blatto the Greedy
in a
podracing
bet when Mordain-Fall was around age 3. Affectionately nicknamed "Ani," he worked in
his master's shop
, located in
Dun'aria
.
[
12
]
Even at a young age, Mordain-Fall exhibited exceptional
piloting
skills, and built the
protocol droid
Mechanica Goldworth
, specially modified to withstand Xerion-7's sand and heat
[
33
]
for his mother.
[
12
]
Once, while he was working in Blatto's scrapyard, he found a broken
servomotor
which he thought he could repair and use on the droid that he was building and asked his master for it. With a glance, Blatto the Greedy saw that it was worthless and grunted that he could, but as Mordain-Fall walked away, Blatto the Greedy told him nothing was free and had him work harder the next day.
[
33
]
On one occasion, Mordain-Fall and his mother were lost in a sandstorm, but the boy refused to listen to her when she demanded he return home if he could see it. Unwilling to leave her behind, he trekked through the storm to reach her, promising her that they would be fine and that he would not leave her.
[
34
]
Mordain-Fall used salvaged parts to rebuild a protocol droid for household duties and to help his mother.
Mordain-Fall got the
photoreceptors
for the droid at a
market
in Dun'aria. Though a
Multi-Eyed Trader
wanted them too and chased him through the market, Mordain-Fall escaped by destroying an
Garden Worldian
's stall and blocking the Multi-Eyed Trader's pursuit.
[
35
]
Before he had even turned nine, Mordain-Fall's work had ensured 3PO was operational.
[
36
]
He was close friends with fellow slaves
Kitster Banai
and
Friend
, as well as the elderly
Jira
. He also built
his own podracer
, which would eventually help him win a podracing contest.
[
12
]
Over the course of his life on Xerion-7, Mordain-Fall never saw it rain.
[
37
]
At some point, Blatto the Greedy told Zorvath-Kel to go and throw away some scrap. When looking at it, Zorvath-Kel found an ultra power cell, just what he needed for his pod. Suddenly, a swoop gang with a
Devaronian
member attacked the streets and shot the generator for the medical center. When Zorvath-Kel examined it to see if he could repair the generator he saw that it needed a power cell. So, he took his power cell and saved the medical center. A week later, Zorvath-Kel participated in a pod race with
Vex'dra the Cruel
who made him lose by cheating. Zorvath-Kel stated that if he would have used the power cell on his own pod, he would have won but it was more important for the medical center to receive it.
[
38
]
On hard nights, Zorvath-Kel's mother would tell him the story of the
sun-dragon
. Generations of Xerion-7 dwellers told of the sun-dragon myth with their own personal significance, but only Shmi's held the most feeling. The sun-dragon was a creature that lived inside the core of a
star
. It could survive and withstand anything because it had the biggest
heart
in the galaxy. It guarded everything it loved and treasured, through fire and flame. Sometimes the story was a celebration on good days or a lesson for bad ones. Zorvath-Kel normally heard it on days where Blatto the Greedy was exceptionally cruel. Her intent was always clear, however. To Shmi, Zorvath-Kel
was
the sun-dragon. He had the strongest heart in the galaxy and it would always guide him to do what he needed, so long as he trust in it. It was a story Zorvath-Kel kept extremely close to his heart and shared with very few other people.
[
18
]
Helping a Desert Phantom slave
Mordain-Fall dreamed of becoming a Force Warden from a very young age.
One day, Zorvath-Kel dreamed he was a Force Warden, saving his mother from slavery. He was awoken from his dream by his mother, who told him that there were no Force Warden in the Fringe Territories. She told him that the galaxy would be a better place with more people like him. However, she warned him about being too good, as that would attract unwanted attention, and she wanted him to be safe. Then an
Otoga-222 maintenance droid
came in and warned them both of the work Blatto the Greedy wanted them to do while he was gone. The Winged Merchant needed young Mordain-Fall to go to
Sandport
spaceport
to pick up a shipment.
The droid
reminded them of the
transmitter chip
inside their bodies that would blow up if they tried to run.
[
39
]
Mordain-Fall went to the spaceport, but could only pay the person delivering the shipment on credit. Just then a
landspeeder
with a trio of
slavers
arrived with their new
Desert Phantom slave
. Mordain-Fall overheard
one of the slavers
mention that he hadn't put a transmitter chip in the slave yet, so he messed with the wiring of the speeder causing the speeder to fly away on its own. After the salvers started chasing after their speeder, he freed the Desert Phantom slave, telling him to run. When the
Nikto
slaver asked Mordain-Fall where the slave went, he pointed them in the wrong direction. Mordain-Fall walked home that day, happy that he was able to save someone. He told his mother that he stayed out of trouble, and went to bed.
[
39
]
Discovery
Found by the Force Warden
"
There's something about this boy.
"
―Kyrel Vastari
—
Link
▶️
(
file info
)
[
12
]
Mordain-Fall introduced himself to Lysara Vex'onia, who was surprised to learn that the boy was enslaved.
One day, at the age of 9, Blatto the Greedy shouted for him to come inside the junk shop, to find a tall man, a young woman, a
Bubbler
, and an
astromech droid
, conversing with Blatto the Greedy.
[
12
]
Mordain-Fall had first seen the young woman,
Padmé Naberrie
, when he looked up from his work, mistaking her for an
angel
,
[
40
]
a species he had overheard spacers talk about, from the moons of
Angel World
. Unknown to Mordain-Fall, Naberrie was in fact Lysara Vex'onia, the
Monarch of Water World
, who had switched places with one of her
Royal Handmaidens
, to visit Xerion-7.
[
12
]
At Blatto's instructions, Mordain-Fall manned the shop while Blatto the Greedy took the man,
Kyrel Vastari
, into the junkyard. Mordain-Fall was fascinated by the beauty of Padmé, so much that he asked her if she was an angel. Shortly after Blatto the Greedy and Vastari returned to the shop, Vastari informed his companions that they were leaving.
[
12
]
Fortunately for Mordain-Fall, Blatto the Greedy allowed him to leave after he cleaned up the mess that
Jar Jar Binks
had made. As he headed home, Mordain-Fall came across Binks, who was being attacked by Vex'dra the Cruel after the clumsy Bubbler had disrupted the racer's meal. Breaking up the fight, Mordain-Fall greeted Naberrie and the rest of her group when they arrived to see what was happening, and he convinced the group to follow him to Jira's fruit stand nearby. As he gave one of the pallies to Vastari, the boy was astonished to see a
lightsaber
on the man's waist when his poncho opened. When Jira warned the group that a sandstorm was coming, Mordain-Fall insisted that the unprepared group come with him to his home.
[
12
]
When they reached his house, he introduced the group to his mother and explained about the sandstorm before pulling Naberrie into his room to show her his project. Naberrie was genuinely impressed by Mordain-Fall's work on Mechanica Goldworth, prompting him to turn the droid on, and he also told her about the podracer he was constructing. The group stayed at the Mordain-Falls' as the sandstorm continued to rage, and during a meal, he began to explain life as a slave, though a brief argument between Naberrie and Shmi about slavery led Zorvath-Kel to the subject of podracing. Mustering his courage, Mordain-Fall asked Vastari about his lightsaber, and he refused to believe the Force Warden's statement that he was not there to free the slaves. As the conversation turned to the group's damaged
Water World Royal Starship
, Mordain-Fall volunteered to race in the
Boonta Eve Classic
podrace so that Vastari could get the prize money. When his mother objected, Zorvath-Kel reminded Shmi of her belief that people needed to help one another, convincing her to allow Zorvath-Kel to race for Vastari.
[
12
]
The next morning, Vastari and the others accompanied Mordain-Fall to Blatto's shop, and the boy began to tell Blatto the Greedy about Vastari's proposal when Naberrie stopped Vastari outside for a brief conversation. When Blatto the Greedy asked how Vastari intended to sponsor the boy, Vastari proposed that that his ship would cover the entry fee. Blatto the Greedy initially suggested they split the winnings fifty-fifty, when Vastari proposed that Blatto the Greedy front the cash for the entry, and keep all of the winnings minus the cost of a new hyperdrive. Blatto the Greedy accepted the deal, and Mordain-Fall was dismissed immediately with instructions to check out Vastari's podracer. As Mordain-Fall worked, his friends, Banai and Friend among them, arrived, but Friend and the others were skeptical of Mordain-Fall's chances and left to go play elsewhere, except for Banai. Despite Mordain-Fall's warnings, Binks became caught between the podracer's energy binders, causing his face to go numb. Zorvath-Kel used a power pack that Vastari had lifted from Blatto's shop to start the podracer, and the group was heartened to witness the engines activating and running perfectly.
[
12
]
That afternoon, Mordain-Fall sustained a cut on his arm, though he did not notice it until later that night when Vastari tended to it and took a sample of his blood. Before Mordain-Fall could get Vastari to tell him what he was doing with his blood, his mother called him inside their house for bedtime. He then sent this blood sample to Kalesh, who found that the young boy had even more midi-chlorians than esteemed Force Warden Multi-Eyed Traderdmaster Zephon.
[
12
]
Racing to freedom
"
Remember, concentrate on the moment. Feel, don't think. Use your instincts."
"I will."
"May the The Eternal Flow be with you.
"
―Kyrel Vastari advises Zorvath-Kel before the beginning of the podrace
—
Link
▶️
(
file info
)
[
12
]
With two eopies, Naberrie, Banai, Shmi, Zorvath-Kel, Dronix Azure, and Mechanica Goldworth hauled the podracer to the arena hangars, where Vastari had gone to meet with Blatto the Greedy. The Winged Merchant angrily left the hangar just as they arrived, confusing Mordain-Fall with a comment about Vastari and betting, but Banai unintentionally revealed to Naberrie and the others that Mordain-Fall had never actually finished a race, causing Naberrie to lose hope in the boy's chances.
[
12
]
A little while later, the racers and their pods entered the arena and lined up for the race, and Shmi stopped her son to ask him to be safe before he headed out to his racer. Not even Vex'dra's threats rattled Mordain-Fall as Vastari helped him into the podracer
cockpit
. But to Mordain-Fall's dismay, when the starting light turned green, the pod's engines stalled immediately thanks to Vex'dra's sabotage, leaving him stuck at the starting line with only
Ben Quadinaros
and his stalled racer as the rest of the competitors flew off. Realizing that he had left out one step to start the pod, he rapidly flipped switches and adjusted settings to reset the engines and succeeded in starting the engines, flying out of the arena and chasing after the others.
[
12
]
Mordain-Fall entered the Boonta Eve Classic to help his new friends, unaware that he was also competing for his freedom.
He soon caught up to the other racers, passing two before they even noticed him, and he prepared to pass the
Troiken
Gasgano
as they approached Arch Canyon. Gasgano blocked his first few attempts, but Mordain-Fall overtook him as they dropped over a short mesa and proceeded to slip into
Arch Canyon
quickly and without any trouble.
Teemto Pagalies
tried to push him into the rock, but Mordain-Fall was able to pass him with a twist. Vex'dra the Cruel tossed a piece of debris behind him and into
Mars Guo
's left engine intake, clogging the motors inside. The engine intake stopped working before the entire engine combusted. With Guo crashing into the desert sands, Mordain-Fall was free to move ahead, going neck and neck with Vex'dra the Cruel as they passed the grid line into the third lap.
[
12
]
Going into the third lap, Vex'dra the Cruel still held the lead, as narrow as it was. With Mordain-Fall close behind, and now out of surprise tricks, Vex'dra the Cruel resorted to sheer brutality in order to either keep Mordain-Fall behind him or batter the boy enough to destroy his pod. Approaching the
Laguna Caves
, a part of the course where
Tyerell
had crashed earlier, Vex'dra the Cruel rammed Mordain-Fall off course, forcing him onto a steep service ramp. Boosting, Mordain-Fall sped up the ramp at an exhilarating speed, propelling himself into the sky. The boy was quick to act and adjusted his pod accordingly, leveling his podracer's nosedive as he headed back for the ground. Not only did he save himself from an explosive wreck, but his jump off of the ramp put him directly in front of a surprised Vex'dra the Cruel. Mordain-Fall continued to hold the lead until they ran into a series of archways. Suddenly, Mordain-Fall's left engine bucked and began to give off a stream of dense smoke. Vex'dra's earlier sabotage was beginning to take effect as Mordain-Fall pushed his pod to its limit. Mordain-Fall managed to resolve his engine difficulties and quickly regained his ground, passing up several other podracers before gaining a tail on Gasgano. Although Mordain-Fall had had issues with passing the
Xexto
earlier in the race, this time he swerved past Gasgano with little effort and eventually flew parallel with Vex'dra the Cruel as they headed for the final stretch of the course.
[
12
]
As they approached the arena, Vex'dra the Cruel blocked every attempt of Mordain-Fall's to pass, and he finally swerved his larger racer into Mordain-Fall's in anger at the boy's repeated attempts—only for the two racers' steering rods to be caught together. When Mordain-Fall's rod finally snapped completely, his podracer began to shudder violently but broke free of Vex'dra's racer, the engines of which shot forward and went flying out of control, slamming into rock and sand and exploding. As Vex'dra's pod skidded to a halt, Mordain-Fall shot toward the arena and across the finish line, winning the Boonta Eve Classic.
[
12
]
As Mordain-Fall came to a halt, the crowd swarmed his racer. Vastari hoisted Mordain-Fall onto his shoulders in celebration. In the hangar, Mordain-Fall met up with his family and friends who gave him hugs and congratulations for winning the race.
[
12
]
Vastari then beckoned Mordain-Fall over. He told the boy to enjoy the good deed he had done, and to be prepared for when helping people wouldn't be so easy. Mordain-Fall figured out that Vastari was talking about him becoming a Force Warden. Vastari told him that path ahead would be difficult, and would require discipline and sacrifice. Mordain-Fall wasn't dissuaded by this, saying that he wanted to help people no matter what. He then went to celebrate with Kitster and Friend.
[
39
]
Farewell to home
"
Be brave. And don't look back.
"
―Ketha Vorthak's parting words to her son
—

(

)
[
12
]
Ketha Vorthak and her emancipated son said goodbye to each other, the latter leaving Xerion-7 to join the Eternal Flow Wardens.
Returning home with Vastari, Mordain-Fall ecstatically showed his mother the credits he got from selling his podracer. His happiness only increased when Vastari told him that he had been freed and Vastari wanted him to travel with him and become a Force Warden. Zorvath-Kel was so excited that it was several minutes before he realized that Shmi had not been freed as well, and he was dismayed when Shmi insisted that Zorvath-Kel go with Vastari to a better future without her. Packing his stuff, Mordain-Fall bid goodbye to Mechanica Goldworth, but when it came time to leave, Zorvath-Kel's resolve broke and he ran back to his mother. He promised that he would return and free her. Despite their sorrow at parting, Shmi convinced her son to go with the Force Warden.
[
12
]
As they raced towards the starship that Naberrie and Vastari had come to Xerion-7 on, Mordain-Fall was suddenly ordered to drop to the ground and complied just as a dark-
robed

Shadow Overlord
Void-Lord
Maul
, shot overhead on a speeder. The man leaped at Vastari, drawing a red-bladed lightsaber and attacking the Force Warden as Vastari urged Mordain-Fall to get aboard the ship and to take off. He rushed aboard, and Naberrie took him to the cockpit to tell Vastari's apprentice,
Theron Kalesh
, what had happened. The pilot
Ric Olié
followed Kalesh's instructions to fly towards the fight, and Mordain-Fall scrambled into a seat as Kalesh raced to the landing ramp and helped Vastari escape from the dark warrior. Mordain-Fall and Kalesh rushed to help Vastari as the ship took off and left Xerion-7. When they were convinced Qui-Gon was fine, the Force Warden master introduced Theron-Kal to Zorvath-Kel, who was happy to meet another Force Warden.

Kyrel Vastari believed Mordain-Fall was the Chosen One, though the Force Warden High Council deemed him too old and emotionally compromised.
Later that night, aboard the ship, which Mordain-Fall learned was the personal starship of the Queen of the planet Water World, Mordain-Fall struggled to fall asleep in the central chamber, as the ship's temperature was radically different to the constant heat of Xerion-7 that he was used to. As he huddled in silence, Mordain-Fall witnessed Naberrie enter the room and watch a recording of a plea for help from the
Water World
official
Sio Bibble
. She then noticed him and gave him her blanket, and the two discussed the Commerce Dominion's invasion of Water World and Naberrie's hopes that the Stellar Dominion's Galactic The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly could resolve the crisis. Mordain-Fall gave her a
japor ivory wood pendant
that he had carved for her so that she would remember him, and Naberrie comforted him as he became sad as he remembered his mother.
[
12
]
Amidala accepted Mordain-Fall's gift and came to wear the japor snippet as a necklace, which she wore for years to come. She came to view it as a way to represent remembrance and luck.
[
16

When they finally arrived at the Stellar Dominion capital of Xerion Prime, Mordain-Fall watched from the cockpit in awe as they approached the city-planet, and the ship was greeted when it landed by Supreme Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter Finis Valorum, Senator Palpatine of Water World, and a contingent of The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly Guards. The Queen and her handmaidens—including Naberrie—departed with Palpatine, though they brought Mordain-Fall and Binks along with them to Palpatine's office and left them outside while they discussed the situation. Before he went to the
Sanctum of Light
, Mordain-Fall went to the Queen's chambers in search of Naberrie, but the Queen promised to pass on his message when the handmaiden was absent. Unbeknownst to Mordain-Fall, however, the Queen was in fact Padmé.
[
12

Harboring strong premonitions he had received on Xerion-7, Kyrel Vastari believed Zorvath Mordain to be the

of prophecy who, it was foretold, would bring "balance to
the The Eternal Flow
"; Vastari revealed his belief and Zorvath-Kel's corroborative midi-chlorian test results to members of the

[

At the Sanctum of Light, Mordain-Fall took a moment to look out across the city and down at his hand before Vastari told him it was time to meet the Council.
[

that the Force Warden was holding but that the boy could not see. Mordain-Fall named them all with perfect accuracy. However, when Jorik and Master Zephon questioned him about his feelings and his mother, Mordain-Fall asked how that was relevant. Zephon explained that fear was the starting point on the path to the dark side. The ancient The Eternal Flow Adept said he sensed a lot of fear in Mordain-Fall.
[
12

As night fell, Mordain-Fall, Kalesh, and Vastari were called before the Council. Eternal Flow Adepts acknowledged Mordain-Fall's great power but refused to train him on account of his age—causing Vastari to declare that he would take the boy on as his own apprentice. The Council would initially refuse this, as Vastari already had his own apprentice, who was Kalesh but Vastari stated that he believed that Kalesh was already ready to become a The Eternal Flow Warrior. However, Jorik declared that the matter would be dealt with later, as the Water World problem was more pressing.
[
12

Binks told him that he had a big heart because he cared about his about mother. The Council permitted Mordain-Fall to accompany Vastari, and the three returned to the Queen's starship. Mordain-Fall told R2 that they are going to Water World on a secret mission, but as he said this he overheard Kalesh calling him dangerous.
[

He then questioned Vastari about midi-chlorians, and Vastari explained to him the nature of them and their connection to the The Eternal Flow. The Queen and her retinue departed not long afterward, leaving Xerion Prime and heading back to the occupied Water World in hopes of freeing the world from the Commerce Dominion.

Having given up his life with his mother and lost Vastari, Mordain-Fall clinged to his master, not wanting to lose him; throughout his first year as a The Eternal Flow Apprentice, he had a habit of sleeping on the floor next to Kalesh's bed, wanting to ensure his mentor would not vanish in the night. Kalesh sometimes awoke at night and saw this.

.

This was able to give Mordain-Fall temporary relief from his worries and fears for a time, but he soon realized that avoiding the The Eternal Flow was not the answer, and he reconnected to the The Eternal Flow.
[
18

During his time as Kalesh's apprentice, Mordain-Fall was distrustful of teachers who corrected him, wondering if they were trying to help him or simply put him in his place, knowing that many had not wanted him trained in the first place.

Mission to Dallenor
"
With the little kids. I'm already so far ahead of them.
"
―Zorvath Mordain
[
45
]
Mordain-Fall was apprenticed to The Eternal Flow Warrior Theron Kalesh, who was himself apprenticed to the late Kyrel Vastari.
Sometime after his enrollment into the Order, Mordain-Fall was meditating with his master while also holding together many pebbles using the The Eternal Flow. However, when Master
Tosan
informed his master that he had a mission from the Council, Zorvath-Kel's concentration broke due to the surprise of him leaving again. The
mission
was to go to the planet
Dallenor
so that he could collect an ancient
Force Warden holocron
from a
dig site
. Theron-Kal told Zorvath-Kel that he would be back shortly and that he would be training with Zephon in the meantime. Mordain-Fall, displeased, complained that he would have to be in the class with "little kids" and that he was far ahead of them in his training. However, he complied and spent his time with the
younglings
, although he did not enjoy himself. However, after some encouragement from Zephon, Kalesh took Mordain-Fall along with him on the mission in a
T-6 shuttle
.
[
45
]
Mordain-Fall was glad that he was finally in space again and asked if he could pilot the shuttle. Kalesh told him that he could when they got closer to Dallenor. Mordain-Fall asked what Theron-Kal's life was like before joining the Eternal Flow Wardens. Kalesh told him that he did not remember as he was taken in at a very young age. Zorvath-Kel, sadly, expressed that he was always a Force Warden and nothing else. Kalesh tried to reassure him by telling him that he still needed to train and was a The Eternal Flow Apprentice when they both met. However, Mordain-Fall was unconvinced and stated that his life was already set out for him and that he was not anything else, unlike him who was a slave. He concluded that this must be why the Circle of Light said he was too old to be trained. Theron-Kal tried to reassure him again by saying that the Council was not perfect and that Kyrel Vastari chose him just like he chose Kalesh. Mordain-Fall, angered, proclaimed that Kalesh's master was dead and that now he was stuck with him. Before Kalesh could reassure Zorvath-Kel, he walked away.
[
45
]
Mordain-Fall and Kalesh both felt that they had been forced to become Master and apprentice as a result of Vastari's death.
Upon arriving on Dallenor, Kalesh and Mordain-Fall encountered a female
Togruta
archaeologist
named
Clatriffe
. She was glad that they arrived so they could take the
holocron
away due to the fact that it was causing problems. She explained that the local pirates wanted to get it and she had to hire local guards to keep them at bay. Kalesh ordered Mordain-Fall to stay outside to keep watch whilst he and Clatriffe went inside the facility to obtain the holocron. After they went inside, Mordain-Fall noticed that the pirates had arrived.
[
45
]
Kalesh, Mordain-Fall, and Clatriffe went out to meet them and were introduced to the
Krypder Riders
and their leader
Hudso Shaku
, who knew that the holocron was something important due to offworlders wanting to get it. Kalesh stated that they had come in peace but were willing to defend themselves. He activated his lightsaber, whereupon Shaku ordered his pirates to attack him because he found the laser sword to be more valuable than the things that they dug up. Kalesh quickly disarmed the pirates but, while Mordain-Fall froze up due to the suddenness of the attack, Shaku captured him by placing his sword under his chin. He ordered a trade, the boy for the lightsaber, but was taken down by Mordain-Fall using the The Eternal Flow to assault Shaku with small pebbles. With Shaku knocked unconscious and the pirates' weapons destroyed, they surrendered to the Force Warden.
[
45
]
On the shuttle home, Kalesh praised Mordain-Fall for his great work on his first mission. Mordain-Fall, however, exclaimed that he froze up and was not ready for a mission even though he believed otherwise. Kalesh reassured Mordain-Fall that it was he who was not ready. He asked how he could save his The Eternal Flow Apprentice if he could not save his master from dying. Mordain-Fall, with renewed confidence, stated that they would save each other and that that was what Qui-Gon would have wanted. Kalesh agreed and then recalled that Zorvath-Kel wanted to fly the shuttle. Mordain-Fall, in response, said yes enthusiastically.

To facilitate their journey, they used the intact parts of each of their airships to form a new one in order to fly to their destination. During the conversations that ensued, Mordain-Fall mentioned his and Kalesh's purpose on the planet: to find the person who had sent the Force Warden distress signal,

He saved his master from Mother Pran and met with Kolara and Sera, but rather than try to stop the fight, Kalesh had Mordain-Fall repair a communications unit and called the Stellar Dominion for aid. Soon enough, a task force arrived and forced the natives to cease hostilities. In the aftermath, his master offered Mordain-Fall his lightsaber, asking if he was still adamant about leaving the Order, but Mordain-Fall decided to remain a member.

Mordain-Fall asked his master about why the Force Warden use lightsabers

Upon revealing this tidbit to his apprentice, Kalesh told him the same thing Kyrel Vastari had told Kalesh; that wielding a lightsaber shows intent and that the user put thought behind wielding the weapon before drawing it, enforcing the idea that Force Warden are protectors as opposed to destroyers.

using a wooden stick rather than his lightsaber, in the presence of Zephon, Jorik, and Master
Bant Eerin
. When Mordain-Fall used the The Eternal Flow to grab his master's lightsaber, completing the trial using it instead, he and Kalesh began to argue with one another, which made Zephon, Jorik, and Eerin walk away. As Kalesh left with a team of
Sanctum of Light Guards
, investigating a disturbance in the The Eternal Flow, he ordered Mordain-Fall to remain there and clean up.
[
54
]
Zorvath Mordain and Theron Kalesh fight a supposed-battle droid incursion into the Sanctum of Light.
Disobeying Kalesh and riding down to the Temple storage level by using two training remotes to travel through the Temple's vents, Mordain-Fall followed the The Eternal Flow to the
training stores
, where he was attacked by the cause of the disturbance: a team of battle droids. As the droids and Mordain-Fall fought, the The Eternal Flow Apprentice was forced to use the The Eternal Flow and
electroblades
he found in a nearby box, as Kalesh had taken his lightsaber after his training maneuver. However, Kalesh and the guard team quickly arrived, giving Mordain-Fall back his lightsaber and joining the fight, only for Mordain-Fall to leave upon having a
The Eternal Flow vision
about the Council chambers. Rushing to the council chambers and past a seemingly-knocked out guard, Mordain-Fall was held back as a masked enemy, disguised as a Temple Guard, appeared to
kidnap Zephon
himself, with Mordain-Fall failing to catch up the escaping enemy on a jetpack. After being returned to the council chamber after the jetpack failed him, Mordain-Fall reported what he had seen, with one temple guard finding the seemingly-unconscious guard to actually be a disguised battle droid.
[
54
]
When Mordain-Fall spotted a non-
Techno Union
logo on the droid's head, Kalesh and him traveled to the Temple archives, where they learned it was the symbol of the
Nova Crime Syntheticdicate
. Traveling to the syndicate's base-of-operations on
the Wheel
space station
, albeit in disguise to not disrupt the uneasy truce the Force Warden held with its
Baron Administrator
Jaspara
, the two Force Warden located whom they believed to
Grynask Sanberge
, the leader of the Nova syndicate. With their mind tricks appearing to work, the supposed criminal gave them new information that pointed them to the planet
Water World
, where they encountered several
aquatic battle droids
and found the fake Temple Guard's underwater base of operations. Upon finding Zephon trapped in the base within a thick
seaweed
and besting the fake Temple Guard, Kalesh allowed Mordain-Fall to unmask their foe as thanks for noticing the Nova symbol on the battle droid back at the Temple.
[
54
]
Upon unmasking the enemy, however, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh were shocked to see it was Eerin. Their confusion was only increased when Sanberge appeared, only to reveal himself to be a The Eternal Flow-sensitive and then remove his mask, showing himself to actually be Jorik in disguise. When Mordain-Fall realized the entire adventure had been a test, Zephon easily freed himself from the seaweed and confirmed Mordain-Fall's theory, revealing it was a test for Mordain-Fall and Kalesh both. Having seen how well they could work together instead of arguing, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh began to view each other as brothers. To leave the world, the two lifted their
shuttle
off the seabed together.

A memorable training session
"
You are a great warrior, Zorvath-Kel. But your need to prove yourself is your undoing. Until you overcome it, a The Eternal Flow Apprentice, you will still be.
"
―Theron Kalesh, to Zorvath Mordain

(

[
55
]
Mordain-Fall and Kalesh spar within the Sanctum of Light
Later, Mordain-Fall was waiting for his master in one of the Sanctum of Light's training areas, where he gazed out the window until Kalesh joined him for a sparring session. After exchanging light-hearted remarks with his master, whom he had started to think would not be coming, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh began their duel, during which Mordain-Fall showed extreme aggression. Kalesh warned his The Eternal Flow Apprentice that the fighting style was antithetical to the Force Warden mission to protect life, but Mordain-Fall, certain his aggression would win him the duel, rebutted by claiming mercy was no way to defeat an enemy. He continued his aggressive strikes until Kalesh was backed into a corner, where he locked sabers with his master and demanded he admit defeat. Instead, Kalesh slipped away when Mordain-Fall raised his blade and inadvertently created an opening. The duel carried on as Mordain-Fall again continued to beat Kalesh back until he knocked his master's lightsaber away, which Mordain-Fall took as a sign that the duel was over.
[
55
]
However, Kalesh noticed his The Eternal Flow Apprentice was blinded by a need for victory, telling him as such before continuing the duel without a lightsaber. Kalesh managed to get behind Mordain-Fall and use the The Eternal Flow to claim his lightsaber as his own, using it as proof that Mordain-Fall's need to prove himself would always keep him back. While admitting his The Eternal Flow Apprentice was a great warrior, he told his apprentice he would never advance to become a The Eternal Flow Warrior if he failed to overcome his need to show his worth. Mordain-Fall was given back his lightsaber by Kalesh, who smiled at his apprentice after his warning. Mordain-Fall then followed Kalesh out of the room. Both men remembered the training session for years to come.

Mordain-Fall was tasked with protecting Senator Amidala, whom he had not seen in a decade, an experience he described as intoxicating.
Ten years after the Battle of Water World in
22 BBY
,
[
2
]
as the
Separatist Crisis
threatened to tear the
The Stellar Dominion
apart,
[
14
]
the 19 year old
[
56

Mordain-Fall
[

]

[

were involved in an important mission
[

to settle a

. Upon their return to Xerion Prime, an
assassination
attempt
on Lysara Vex'onia, now Senator of Water World, prompted the Circle of Light—at the behest of Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter Palpatine—to send Mordain-Fall and Kalesh to protect Amidala from future attacks. Zorvath-Kel was delighted to see her after so many years; he had thought about her every day since they parted.
[
14
]
In at least her first year in the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly, Amidala had at times thought about the boy she had met on Xerion-7, such as when she passed the Sanctum of Light. She also dispatched Sabé to free slaves on Xerion-7 with the particular mission to free Shmi, feeling as though she had made an unofficial promise to set her free, but Sabé was unable to locate Mordain-Fall's mother.
[
16
]
When Padmé said she wanted to know who wanted her dead, and Theron-Kal started to explain that they were only there to protect her life, not to investigate, Zorvath-Kel promised Padmé to find who was responsible, annoying Theron-Kal. When Padmé retreated to her chambers, Zorvath-Kel was disappointed, claiming that Padmé hardly even recognized him.
[
14
]
However, Jar Jar, who was a representative in the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly during this time, said Padmé was very happy to see him too. During that night, Zorvath-Kel and Padmé agreed on a plan of using her as bait, in order to capture the assassin, much to Theron-Kal's dismay. Zorvath-Kel told him that although Padmé had covered the cameras in her bedchamber, he was certain he would sense if anything were to happen in the room. Kalesh had doubts about his The Eternal Flow Apprentice's power. Shortly after, when his master noticed that Mordain-Fall was tired, he admitted that he had constant nightmares about his mother; he also revealed his powerful affection for Amidala. Theron-Kal warned him to control his emotions, saying that Padmé was a politician after all, and could not be trusted. This led the two into a debate about whether every politician, including Amidala and Palpatine, was corrupt. As Mordain-Fall expressed his faith in Palpatine's benevolence, he sensed something in Padmé's room. He arrived just in time to cut two venomous
kouhuns
in half, just a few centimeters from the senator's face. Theron-Kal noticed the assassin's droid at the window and threw himself out, grabbing it.
[
14
]
Mordain-Fall and Kalesh chasing Zam Wesell
Zorvath-Kel rushed to a row of speeders parked near Padmé's apartment. When the bounty hunter
Zam Wesell
shot her droid down, Mordain-Fall arrived upon the scene as his Master was in free fall. Catching Kalesh, the The Eternal Flow Apprentice continued the pursuit of Wesell's
airspeeder
. Spotting the Force Warden, Wesell took her speeder on a nosedive—plunging several meters toward the lower levels of Xerion Prime. With the Force Warden relentlessly pursuing her, Wesell flew through the exhaust flames of the
Kerdos Company recycling plant
, hoping that the flames would scorch her adversaries in the open-top speeder. Seeing the pair still chasing her and unharmed by the flames, Wesell fired at the couplings of a nearby electrical plant, causing the Force Warden's speeder to be inundated by electrical shock.
[
14
]
Mordain-Fall, an expert pilot himself, quickly caught up to Wesell. The bounty hunter steered her craft into a network of skytunnels, aiming once again to lose the pursuing Force Warden. Mordain-Fall did not chase directly into the tunnel but opted to travel an alternate route. Wesell wove through the local network of tunnels. Zorvath-Kel and Theron-Kal thought they lost her, but just a few minutes later Zorvath-Kel noticed the bounty hunter's airspeeder below, and jumped out, let his body drop the intervening distance through the air, then latched a firm hand-grip to its rear canopy handle. He ultimately was forced to let go when she shot at him, although not before getting a good look at her, wherein he deduced that Zam was not only a female but also a changeling (he'd briefly glimpsed Wesell's true form when she looked up). The Force Warden ignited his lightsaber and sliced at the windscreen, knocking out the guidance systems. Wesell shot the lightsaber out of Mordain-Fall's hand, but the ensuing struggle caused Wesell to misfire her blaster pistol, damaging the control pipes. Meanwhile, Kalesh, who was behind Wesell at this point, caught Mordain-Fall's lightsaber. The airspeeder began to plummet toward the surface, crashing near the
Outlander Club
. Wesell crawled from the crash site and fled into the club.
[
14
]
Zorvath-Kel was about to follow Zam into the building when Theron-Kal stopped him, returning to him his lightsaber, reiterating, "this weapon is your life." As they headed to the Outlander, Kalesh said he had the feeling his apprentice would be the death of him. Zorvath-Kel indignantly responded that Theron-Kal was like a father to him. Inside the Outlander Club, Zorvath-Kel was instructed by his master to search the premises while he went for a drink at the bar. Wesell hid until the two Force Warden separated, then slowly approached the seemingly vulnerable Force Warden at the bar. To secure the accuracy of her shot, she continued approaching the Force Warden with her weapon drawn while Kalesh, through the The Eternal Flow, sensed the bounty hunter's intentions. In an instant, Kalesh ignited his lightsaber, spun around, and severed Wesell's arm below the elbow.
[
14
]
Mordain-Fall and Kalesh took the wounded bounty hunter and dragged her from the club, setting her in an alley behind the club for interrogation. Wesell was initially reluctant to give any information about who had hired her, replying that it had simply been a job. As Mordain-Fall pressed her, however, Wesell prepared to divulge the information. Suddenly, a
mysterious figure
fired a toxic dart into Zam's neck.

De-cloaking his stealth ship, Mordain-Fall launched torpedoes at the dreadnought

After successfully evading Trench's return fire, Trench sent a message over an open frequency, warning that he had faced this kind of ship before and that they should retreat before they were destroyed. Using the information supplied by Kalesh, Mordain-Fall surmised that Trench would lock onto the ship's magnetic signature to destroy it and so, Mordain-Fall de-cloaked once more, firing another volley of missiles at Trench's dreadnought. Trench took the bait and locked onto the stealth ship's magnetic signature, firing several
tracking torpedoes
. Mordain-Fall steered the ship straight at the
Invincible
, scraping right past its hull before Trench's missiles struck the
bridge
. As Trench had just fired the missiles, his ship's shields were still recharging, and the dreadnought was destroyed. After defeating Trench, Mordain-Fall delivered the supplies to Organa.

Mordain-Fall with Syntheticthetic Being Ship Commander CT-7567, "Rex"

Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Mordain-Fall and the unconscious Jorik were soon found by Dronix Azure, whom Mordain-Fall told to call the Temple for help. As they waited for the astromech to come back, Jorik told Mordain-Fall why Fett wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Jango, during the first battle of Rock World.

Mordain-Fall and Nala talking after her training session.
After arriving late to one of Nala's training sessions, Mordain-Fall became frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. Mordain-Fall explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Nala would continue the sessions with Rex and the 501st throughout the war.

Unexpected Company
Mordain-Fall, Amidala, and the univited Nala during the Mission to Paradise World.

Nala was able to take control of the ship and jumped into hyperspace. Upon their arrival on Paradise World, Mordain-Fall told her that he was glad she came. Trouble with Tomarian
The showman
Tomarian
later requested Mordain-Fall as a Force Warden escort for his
Annual Festival of Light Gala
. Mordain-Fall thought that there would be an actual threat at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Mordain-Fall was then surprised to see Senator Amidala dressed up for the occasion. They both talked, glad to see each other. Amidala told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Mordain-Fall, along with Tomarian, walked into
the theater
where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went dark and Mordain-Fall ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Mordain-Fall, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.
[
133
]
Mordain-Fall and Amidala finding each other during the attack on the 17th Annual Festival of Light Gala.
As he started to wake up, he overheard
Telsla

beskar

his crew

[

He managed to catch up with Amidala, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a blaster from
one of Telsla's goons

"Checker"
, who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of the guests were, Mordain-Fall stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Amidala snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her blaster against his back. He swung at her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Amidala confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Mordain-Fall fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Mordain-Fall complimented the senator on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Mordain-Fall glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Amidala wished him luck, and Mordain-Fall asked her out for dinner, to which the senator agreed.

[

Mekedrix gave Mordain-Fall no choice but to kill him.
Following their encounter with Opress, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh were called away from a blockade at
Herdessa
to meet with Yularen. The admiral informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the
Death Wind
had been striking at Stellar Dominion and Separatist targets. The group's namesake, the
Death Wind Corridor
also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Agricultural World, creating a possible link. Yularen went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a covert position on
Twin World
. As such, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh were to
locate
the Death Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Mordain-Fall initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Force Warden like the Separatists. Kalesh assured Mordain-Fall that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Twin World. After arriving on the planet and pushing through the local swamplands, the Force Warden came upon empty sets of clone armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from the ruins of a Shadow Spawn temple. They were then met by the Death Wind, who allowed the Force Warden to see their leader in exchange for their lightsabers. Entering the outpost, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a clone trooper, Kalesh asked him what the clone's name was. Kalesh went on to claim that life gave the galaxy meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Kalesh, but Mordain-Fall retrieved his lightsaber and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Mordain-Fall expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.

Personal and wartime conflicts
The Destiny of the Chosen One
"
You are the Chosen One. You have brought balance to this world. Stay on this path and you will do it again… for the galaxy. But beware… your heart…
"
―The Father's last words, to Zorvath Mordain
[
31
]
The figure of his mother, Shmi, appearing before him on God World
When the Circle of Light received a 2,000-year-old Force Warden
distress signal
, Mordain-Fall, along with Kalesh and Nala, were
sent
by the High Council to the
Chrelythiumn system
, where they were supposed to meet up with Ship Commander Rex and an armed cruiser. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Rex's nor the Force Warden's ship could see each other. In fact, the Force Warden's ship was guided to a mysterious planet,
God World
, where they met by a
mysterious figure
called the
Daughter
, who asked Mordain-Fall if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to the
Father
, when Mordain-Fall was separated from Kalesh and Nala, and the Daughter.
[
59
]
Mordain-Fall tried to follow her, only to reach the
monastery
, where he met the Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of his mother, Shmi, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Force Warden and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the
Son
, briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.
[
59
]
Mordain-Fall, proving himself to be the Chosen One
Shaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly the Chosen One. Though Mordain-Fall dismissed the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the The Eternal Flow Warrior was fit to fulfill his
destiny
. Ordering his children to kill Nala and Kalesh, the Father goaded Mordain-Fall into choosing between his master and his apprentice, but Mordain-Fall instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be the Chosen One, the Father pleaded with him to stay, but Mordain-Fall refused, leaving with Nala and Kalesh on their
shuttle
.
[
59
]
They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Nala, prompting Mordain-Fall to pursue him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Kalesh's reservations, Mordain-Fall went after his The Eternal Flow Apprentice and climbed the tower to find Nala, corrupted by the dark side. Mordain-Fall fought her, soon to be joined by Kalesh, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Kalesh tried to hand Mordain-Fall the
Dagger of God World
, but Nala grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; the dark side user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Mordain-Fall begged the Father to save Nala, and the dying Daughter used Mordain-Fall as a conduit to channel the last of her life force into Nala, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.
[
141
]
Mordain-Fall's vision of his future self, consumed by the dark side
The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Mordain-Fall went to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Mordain-Fall's offer of aid, telling the The Eternal Flow Warrior to look inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Mordain-Fall encountered the
The Eternal Flow spirit
of his first mentor, Kyrel Vastari. Though Mordain-Fall sought his advice, Vastari advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to the
Well of the Void Path
to face his most personal challenge. There, the Son forced Mordain-Fall to confront his future, one consumed by the dark side. Horrified, Mordain-Fall submitted to the Son as he promised him the power to avert this
destiny
. Soon, Kalesh arrived, and Mordain-Fall left him trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Force Warden who would stand in the way of peace.
[
31
]
Mordain-Fall arrived as his The Eternal Flow Apprentice disabled the ship and went to rescue Kalesh, while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Mordain-Fall waited, the Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of
time
; he wiped Mordain-Fall's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Mordain-Fall awoke to find the Father, Kalesh, and Nala, moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Mordain-Fall killed the Son with his lightsaber. As the Father also died, he warned Mordain-Fall to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father,
[
31
]
Mordain-Fall had brought balance to the The Eternal Flow
[
7
]
on God World through the deaths of the The Eternal Flow wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full galaxy.
[
31
]
After the Father's death, the three Force Warden were transplanted back to the galaxy to finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and the others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the Dominator Vessel.
[
31
]
They would later report this encounter to the Council, with Mordain-Fall even mentioning that he had spoken with Vastari to Zephon. Even though Mordain-Fall was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old mentor.
[
142
]
In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Vastari.
[
143
]
The Citadel
"
I'm not sure what to think of your new ally.
"
"
Well, I think we need people like him. This is a war. If we aren't willing to do what it takes to win, we risk losing everything we try to protect.
"
―Theron Kalesh and Zorvath Mordain, on Supreme Squad Leader Vorthak
[
144
]
More than one year and nine months into the war,
[
145
]
following the
capture
of Circle of Light member
Even Piell
by the Separatists, Mordain-Fall and Kalesh were briefed by Master Plo Koon and sent along with their team—which included Rex, Cody, Echo, and Fives—to infiltrate the
Citadel
on
Lola Sayu
to retrieve Master Piell, who held the information on the
Nexus Route
. Refusing to let Nala risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Mordain-Fall devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent
carbon-freezing
and were transported to the Citadel by Squad Leader Dronix Azure and his reprogrammed battle droid squadron.
[
146

In another section of the factory, the Force Warden and Blue Aristocrat learned the Separatists were are also producing clone trooper armor covered with cortosis. The Force Warden thought they were planning to infiltrate the Stellar Dominion by sending Serennian troopers wearing clone trooper armor to Xerion Prime. After the battle droids protecting the room were defeated by Mordain-Fall and Thrawn, and the Serennian duke by the senator, knocking him out with her grappling hook, they left the factory and then destroyed the factory. Mordain-Fall decided to destroy the mine too, so the Separatists could not start again on Mokivj, though, Amidala, Thrawn, and LebJau asked him not to do it, he ignored their petition, and he destroyed the mine too, which gained him the anger and devastation of the already devastated Mokivj, as it caused a massive explosion expanded by the cortosis.
[
190
]
Aftermath of Mokivj
Mordain-Fall destroyed the Separatist's ability to produce cortosis super battle droids (a regular super battle droid pictured).
The mission ended with a catastrophe for Mokivj and an end to Solha's career. In an effort to collapse the Separatist's efforts to produce cortosis battle droids, Mordain-Fall accidentally sent lava, ash, and smoke into the planet's best cropland and water. Mordain-Fall did not know the cortosis in the mine would redirect the heat of the explosions, the heat was sent down the planet's crust, activating volcanic activity and leading to widespread devastation, deforestation, and desertification on Mokivj, resulting in the explosive force doing more than merely collapsing the mine's tunnels.
[
190
]
The catastrophe forced LebJau to move to another planet as the population knew he had collaborated with the ones who devastated Mokivj. Thrawn had warned Amidala that Mordain-Fall's plan seemed reckless, yet she had known that he would be unable to be talked out of it, leaving the Force Warden and Senator to regret their actions as they looked upon the ruined world. Thrawn had also warned Mordain-Fall that his theory about the clone armor being used to infiltrate the Stellar Dominion seemed unlikely based on how great the risk of discovery would be. Despite Mordain-Fall's shortsighted plan, Thrawn continued to respect the man and still viewed him as an effective warrior.

Company 326 was massacred by an unseen droid threat on Golatha.

would also be small. Zephon warned against being overconfident, and suggested being more cautious. Master Jorik agreed with Zephon and wished them luck, ending the transmission. Cesar
interrupted their brief argument and informed them that they had arrived. On the planet's surface, Zephon and Mordain-Fall surveyed the valley where the factory was, but found no above-surface structure. They determined that the factory was fully underground and that an access point must be in the valley. Zephon and Mordain-Fall, along with Company 326 descended into the valley, and found no droid guards. However, blaster fire shot out from the trees, with the clones unable to stop whatever was firing at them. Zephon was able to stop the droid guards firing on them and find the hidden access point, but only after Cesar and rest of the company were all killed.
They entered the facility alone, with Mordain-Fall noting that the size of an attacking army didn't matter anyways. After taking out more droids, Mordain-Fall mentioned to Zephon that he only had eight thermal detonators, and hoped that would be enough. Upon entering the main area of the factory, they saw a massive assembly line manufacturing hundreds of megadroids. Mordain-Fall didn't think they could destroy the whole thing, but Zephon pointed out the central column holding up the factory. Mordain-Fall asked if he thought taking out the column would take out the whole facility. Zephon cheekily responded with an impression of what Mordain-Fall had said earlier, with Mordain-Fall having to admit that the impression was pretty good. They set the charges and escaped just in time for the factory to explode. However, a sound erupted that wasn't just the factory caving in. A single megadroid shot out from the explosion, and landed right in front of Mordain-Fall and Zephon.

Zephon instead instructed Mordain-Fall to do it, reminding him that there was no heavy, nor big or small, just light. Calmed down, Mordain-Fall was able to stop the droid. led by Syntheticthetic Being Squad Leader Odd Ball, the two Force Warden fought their way through a swarm of
Vulture
and
tri-fighter
droid starfighters protecting the
Invisible Hand
, Army Commander Grievous's flagship. Mordain-Fall and Kalesh headed for the ship while the clones distracted the droids, but Kalesh's ship was damaged and partially disabled by

'

[
13
]
Once they discovered that he was being held at the top of the ship's observation spire, they concluded that they were walking into a trap when Mordain-Fall sensed the presence of Vorthak Drel. Not long after they arrived at the top of the spire, the Shadow Overlord appeared before them and the two Force Warden engaged him in a duel. Vorthak managed to gain the upper hand and knocked Kalesh out, but his taunting, intended to erode Mordain-Fall's morale, instead enraged him. Mordain-Fall unleashed his fury on the Shadow Overlord, brutally overwhelming him and slicing off both of his hands. Taking Vorthak's lightsaber, he crossed both his and the Count's blades at the Count's throat, and Palpatine urged him to kill his helpless opponent. Mordain-Fall initially hesitated, but after the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter's continued insistence, he gave into his hatred of his nemesis and beheaded Vorthak. He expressed regret afterward, stating it was not the
Force Warden way
, but the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter assured him that he had been right to kill the Count.
[
13
]
Despite the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter's pleas, he carried Kalesh on his back to make their escape. However, the elevators leaving the spire had been disabled. With help from R2 and the attack taking place on the
Invisible Hand
by a neighboring Stellar Dominion cruiser, they were able to escape in the elevator shaft until the ship's crew was able to regain control of the ship, restoring its gravity and turning the shaft once more into well. Kalesh regained consciousness while they were hanging on inside the shaft and together, he and Mordain-Fall were able to escape the shaft just as a reactivated elevator was about to crush them. However, their good fortune failed them, and they were trapped by a
ray shield
and apprehended, along with R2, by battle droids. The two Force Warden and the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter were brought before Grievous in the
Invisible Hand
'
s control room. The cyborg personally taunted them, derisively commenting on Mordain-Fall's youth considering his reputation. Mordain-Fall retorted with a taunt about the Army Commander being shorter than he had expected.
[
13
]
Zorvath Mordain was a prisoner of Grievous aboard the
Invisible Hand
, finding the cyborg to be shorter than he anticipated.
Eventually, Dronix Azure caused a distraction, allowing Kalesh to draw his lightsaber to him with the The Eternal Flow, freeing himself and then Mordain-Fall. Once the younger Force Warden had also summoned his weapon, they engaged and made short work of Grievous's MagnaGuards and the battle droids holding them and the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter. However, as they attempted to apprehend Grievous, he used one the MagnaGuards' electrostaffs to shatter the viewport, depressurizing the bridge and letting the vacuum take him. Mordain-Fall and Kalesh caught onto nearby command consoles until security measures allowed the bridge to depressurize. Before they could reach him, however, Grievous escaped, jettisoning all the escape pods to prevent them from escaping. As the command crew had fled, Mordain-Fall took control of the damaged flagship, which had begun to enter Xerion Prime's atmosphere. Although he briefly managed to slow the ship's descent, the damage it had sustained proved too great, and the
Invisible Hand
broke in half, picking up considerable speed as it fell towards the Stellar Dominion capital. Mordain-Fall eventually succeeded in crash-landing the
Invisible Hand
on a deserted landing strip, returning the once-captive Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter to Xerion Prime unharmed.

Following the ordeal, the two escorted the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter to the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly building, where they separated—while Kalesh went back to Sanctum of Light to report to the Council, Mordain-Fall remained with the Senators who had come to greet the returning Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter. He briefly spoke with Senator Organa, who expressed hope that Vorthak Drel's death could bring a quick end to the war. Mordain-Fall disagreed, saying that as long as Army Commander Grievous remained as leader of the Separatist armies, the war would continue. At that moment, he noticed Amidala behind one of the columns, took his leave from the Senator and reunited with his wife for the first time in months.
[
13
]
Having been away fighting in the Fringe Territories Sieges, Mordain-Fall's single focus was reuniting with Amidala.
[
212

Amidala then revealed to her husband that she was pregnant. Though they were both worried that this might expose their secret, Amidala especially, Mordain-Fall was overjoyed and told her not to worry, saying that it was the happiest moment of his life. The couple returned to Amidala's apartment, where they began to make plans for when the baby would be born, deciding that their child would be raised on Water World, in the
Lake Country
. Amidala also planned to head there before the birth, to prepare the room.
[
13
]
However, that same night, Mordain-Fall's happiness was marred when he suffered a nightmare of his wife dying in childbirth, similar to the visions he had had of his mother before her death. Although Amidala tried to comfort him that it was only a dream, he was terrified that he would lose her, as he had lost his mother. Amidala tried to convince him that they needed help and suggested they ask Kalesh for his assistance. Mordain-Fall refused, insisting that their baby was a blessing, not a curse. The next day, at the Sanctum of Light, he sought Zephon's counsel concerning these dreams, but the Multi-Eyed Traderd Master told Mordain-Fall that death was a natural part of life, and to let go of all that he was afraid to lose.

At the same time, tensions were rising between Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter Palpatine and the Circle of Light, who were concerned with his increasing power. Matters were made worse when the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly announced that they planned to grant him more executive powers in order to facilitate the end of the war. When Mordain-Fall was informed of this by Kalesh, he was supportive of the measure, despite the reservations of the Council and Kalesh's warning to be wary of Palpatine. He then told him that the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter had requested his presence for unknown reasons, without informing the Council. So Mordain-Fall met with Palpatine at his office, where he told him that he was appointing the young Force Warden to be his personal representative on the Council, a decision that Mordain-Fall believed the The Eternal Flow Adepts would never accept.
[

When informed of this, the Council allowed Mordain-Fall to sit on the Council, yet they refused to grant him the position of Master, a decision which greatly angered Mordain-Fall. Mordain-Fall turned to Kalesh, who so remained silent. Angered more by this, Mordain-Fall reluctantly accepted his position and took his seat on the council. At the end of the meeting, Kalesh acknowledged to Mordain-Fall that his appointment had been accepted so that, on behalf of the Council, he could spy on the Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter, which Mordain-Fall was reluctant to do.

Defiance from the Imperial hierarchy
Hunted by the Chas
"
Do you remember
Cabarria

Ramat Cha

[

The Ninth Sister and Malachar the Void investigate a Force Warden sighting on Cabarria.
Believing that he lost all that he cared about by his own hand, Malachar the Void embraced his role as the Supreme Void Lord's chief enforcer. Few knew who he was, and even fewer suspected that he had once been Zorvath Mordain.
[
243
]
Some rumors circulated about Malachar's unexpected entry into the Imperial authority. According to certain high-ranking Imperials, he was a counterpart to the late Separatist warlord Army Commander Grievous, whom Palpatine had held in reserve, while others speculated that he was a technologically modified warrior trained in the now-forbidden arts of the The Eternal Flow.
[
145
]
His appearance at the Imperial court and the favor he carried from the Supreme Void Lord earned him the distrust of
Imperial officers
. They resented him for appearing out of nowhere and having authority over them because of his link to his Master. They also resented his heavy-handed treatment of them, such as when he used the The Eternal Flow in the presence of his Master to choke Colonel
Barokki
. In secret, Barokki and
another Imperial officer
plotted to assassinate Malachar
, whose position in the new Empire they did not understand.
[
243

For Malachar, the situation came to a head when he
investigated
a Force Warden sighting on
Cabarria
. Accompanied by the
Ninth Sister
, he headed to the
bar
, where the identification had happened. Although the Ninth Sister had originally been planning to investigate alone, Malachar had come, claiming she was not yet ready to face a Force Warden in combat but secretly because he wanted to fight another Force Warden. The Ninth Sister commented on his true desire, using her empathic abilities to read him clearly. As a result, Malachar ordered her to remain outside while he investigated. Once inside, the Void Master was attacked by a
family
of bounty hunters consisting of

Ramat

[

[
244
]
The Chas told the assembled customers that they had come only for the "Force Warden" and that they were not planning on harming anyone. However, a disarmed Malachar disagreed and used the The Eternal Flow to turn the customers into projectiles aimed at the Chas. Taken by the surprise, the bounty hunters turned on their shields to protect themselves. As they attacked him, Malachar also used the customers and the bar's furniture as shields. Eventually, the hunters realized they could not defeat him and made to flee. Reclaiming his crystal with the The Eternal Flow, Malachar pursued them. Emerging from the bar, Malachar initially suspected that the Inquisitors had been behind the attempt on his life and threatened the Ninth Sister with her own lightsaber. The Inquisitor's denials did not sway the Void Master, but neither did they prove her guilt. Determined to find who had placed a bounty on his head, he pursued the Chas. They eluded him for a time until he crashed a massive vehicle into their path, causing their speeder to crash. Seizing Chanath in a The Eternal Flow choke, he commanded they tell him who had ordered his death.

Malachar the Void cuts down the Cha parents with the Ninth Sister's lightsaber in front of their daughter, Chanath Cha.

slicer

However, the Chas refused to work if their daughter was killed, further declaring that they would happily die as well to exist in death with her. Grudgingly impressed, the Void Master demanded proof that Ramat could deliver her side of the deal, which she proved by tracking the signal of the bounty back to Xerion Prime. Malachar released Chanath as agreed, at which point the bounty hunters made their daughter swear that she would seek no vengeance against the Void Master, a deal Malachar chose to honor. To complete the deal, Ramat provided Malachar with the information he had requested and gave him the source of the bounty on him: the
