Cementing his place
"
Still, I understand that our shared vision remains new, and perhaps some elements of the hierarchy remain unclear. It is time to elimate any confusion. This is Lord Malachar. He speaks with my voice. A command from him is a command from me. Pass this information down to your men. All must understand.
"
"
Ferro. Bingan. Strephi. Azoras. Barokki. Step forward. Two attempts have been made on my life. Evidence suggests a conspiracy within the Imperial Officer Corps. I do not yet know the precise individuals behind these attempts… although I have my suspicions. It does not matter. I will always survive. Every time, I will survive. But five of you, chosen at random…
[The Eternal Flow chokes the five]
will not.
"
―Supreme Void Lord Palpatine and Malachar the Void speak to the Imperial Officer Corps
[
243
]
Faced with a conspiracy to assassinate him, Malachar the Void used his skills as a pilot to avoid incoming missiles.
With their business on Cabarria completed, Malachar and the Ninth Sister left the planet and returned to Xerion Prime. Now suspecting that the Supreme Void Lord had placed the bounty on his head, the Void Master planned to confront his Master about his intentions. As the Ninth Sister piloted the ship through hyperspace, Malachar worked on creating a new lightsaber. His
droid
meanwhile investigated the origins of the kill contract placed on him and confirmed that the order could have come from the Supreme Void Lord's office, with no evidence of slicing to indicate foul play. But, as the ship arrived in orbit over Xerion Prime, the ship's attempt to transmit clearance codes for landing was jammed and the ship itself was marked as being part of a
Separatist splinter cell
. The Imperial capital's orbital defenses targeted Malachar's ship. Only the Void Master's skill as a pilot prevented them from being destroyed, although the ship was damaged and forced to crash land on Xerion Prime. Tired of the repeated
attempts
on his life, the Void Master headed to the former Sanctum of Light to confront the Supreme Void Lord.
[
243
]
The Supreme Void Lord was investigating the hoard of artifacts that Jocasta Nu had collected and stored in the The Knowledge Vault when Malachar arrived. The Supreme Void Lord sensed that his apprentice did not wish to discuss his recent discovery, although he did praise him for having found the Librarian's stash. Malachar informed his master of the events on Cabarria and his investigation into the kill order against him, before directly asking his Master whether he had placed it on his head as part of a test. Noticing Malachar's new lightsaber, the Supreme Void Lord asked to see it, approving of its design. He also chastised his apprentice for believing that he would resort to such methods as placing a bounty on his head if he wanted to have him killed and that he did not "test": he "taught." He also told Malachar that finding the assassins was his task, not theirs. This echoed a previous lesson he had taught Malachar when he had "disciplined" Colonel Barokki: that they must not kill everyone in the galaxy, but that having power meant they could, and that they would never lack for people to destroy.
[
243
]
Malachar returned to his quarters, where he meditated on the past events and his Master's words. Thinking back to all that had happened, he eventually realized the cause of the conspiracy against him was that his sudden elevation had caused concern and fear among the ranks of the Imperial officers, who knew that he had "strength," but not that he shared the Supreme Void Lord's "power." Despite a lack of proof, Malachar became convinced that Colonel Barokki, who had so suddenly borne his displeasure, must be involved in the conspiracy. The Void Master met with the Supreme Void Lord and shared his conclusions, and requested that he be publicly recognized and allowed to show them the consequences of opposing him. The Supreme Void Lord asked whether he was sure, and Malachar confirmed it: he would not kill them all unless he had to, but would not allow them to act against him with impunity. The Supreme Void Lord agreed to Malachar's plan, but forbade him from killing Vorthak, as he was essential to his plans.
[
243
]
Malachar the Void meditates in his suite after establishing his place in the Imperial Military.
The Supreme Void Lord gathered the
elite officers
of the growing Imperial Military to clarify the new hierarchy. He formally introduced Malachar as his emissary, who spoke with his voice and whose commands would need to be obeyed as if they had come from the Supreme Void Lord himself. The Supreme Void Lord then moved aside and allowed Malachar to speak: the Void Master summoned five officers before him:
Corin Ferro
,
Zorta Bingan
,
Joon Strephi
,
Tomas Azoras
, and Barokki. Informing them that the two attempts on his life had been made from within the Officer Corps and that he had his suspicions who had been responsible, he instituted a new rule: for every assassination attempt he survived, and he warned them that he would
always
survive, five officers chosen at random would be executed. He instantly executed the five designated officers with a The Eternal Flow choke before departing, leaving the remaining officers stunned and afraid. With his position in the hierarchy established,
[
243
]
Malachar did not need to hold an official military rank. Instead, he acted as the de facto
Squad Leader-in-Chief
of the Imperial Military
[
247
]
as per his position as the Supreme Void Lord's right hand man.
[
243
]
Malachar would also come to be recognized as part of both the
Joint Chiefs of the Imperial Military
and the
Imperial Ruling Council
, of which he was considered a ranking member.
[
23
]
After his demonstration before the officer corps, Malachar returned to his personal suite and mediated, cracking the window in his anger that positive thoughts still came.

War on Mon Cala
Malachar awakes from his meditation
In
18 BBY
,
[
2
]
one year into the reign of the Empire, Malachar was reliving his duel on Lava World through meditation. During his experience, he was already in his cybernetic suit, and he used the The Eternal Flow to throw lava at Theron-Kal. Theron-Kal jumped up to dodge it, but Malachar The Eternal Flow-choked him and threw him into the rocks. Malachar watched Theron-Kal burn before he awoke in his meditation chamber as the Supreme Void Lord asked for him over the comm. In his throne room, the Supreme Void Lord told Malachar that a year after his ascension, it was time to show that his voice was the only voice within the Empire. Malachar asked how, and the Supreme Void Lord told him that Mon Cala would make a great example of the price of defiance. He further told Malachar that
King
Lee-Char may have had an adviser helping him with his negotiations with the Empire. Malachar asked if it could be a Force Warden, and the Supreme Void Lord said it was possible. He told Malachar to take a team to investigate this and claimed the military aspects of the operation would be left to Moff Vorthak.

Zeta

Telvar

Chief of Security
, Gial Ackbar, and Malachar confronted the Ocean World, asking if he had read the writ that the Ninth Sister had given him. Ackbar confirmed that he had, but demanded that he knew their business as he had a job to do and so did Malachar. Malachar complied and explained about a possible enemy of the Empire on Mon Cala. As he said that, Telvar's shuttle took off and flew for Vorthak's Dominator Vessel, the
Sovereign
—which was in orbit above Mon Cala. However, as Ackbar denied the possibility of an enemy of the Empire being on Mon Cala, Telvar's shuttle exploded.

Malachar and his Inquisitors seize the landing pad.
Telvar's assassination prompted Vorthak to enact a full-scale

to find Lee-Char and ask him about the possible Force Warden. They fought their way through the palace guards and entered the palace, where the Ninth Sister began torturing Lee-Char for the location of the Force Warden. However, another threat loomed over them. A massive tidal wave had suddenly erupted as a part of Lee-Char's defensive plan. The Sixth Brother warned them of this, and he and the Tenth Brother began shielding Malachar and the Ninth Sister from the wave using the The Eternal Flow. When their attempts began failing, Malachar joined them, but it was not enough. The group was washed away by the wave, and Mon Calamari City was left in ruins.

Ferren Barr discovered that Malachar the Void was once Zorvath Mordain, the prophesied Chosen One of the Eternal Flow Wardens.

Investigation on Rock World

There had been certain instances of sabotage during the Oblivion Orb's construction that Malachar needed to discover the source of before it got worse. The Supreme Void Lord further told Malachar that Vorthak had full authority over him and that if he disappointed him, he disappointed Palpatine.

Malachar visits Galen Erso's office.

The Imperial officers remained wary of Malachar and his power, as well as his continued devotion to the ancient ways of the The Eternal Flow, but none rose to challenge him without feeling the consequences.
[

For two decades, Malachar was the loyal enforcer of Supreme Void Lord Sheev Palpatine, commanding the might of the Imperial Army as well.
Despite the mistrust and even hatred he generated among the officers, Malachar was held in high esteem by the
Stormtrooper Corps
, as he often fought alongside them at the front line rather than remaining in command centers. As the Supreme Void Lord's enforcer, Malachar retained command of the unit he had commanded during the The Phantasm Wars as Zorvath Mordain: the Iron Phantom Corps. When the Supreme Void Lord retired the clone troopers, the rank and file of the 501st were composed of recruited stormtroopers, some of whom received training from the Void Master himself, such as Squad Member
Kreel
, an undercover agent whom Malachar trained in lightsaber combat. Through their missions on Malachar's behalf, the 501st came to be known as "Malachar's Fist."

Malachar surveyed Uokara, a planet in the Forbidden Spheres, for signs of life on behalf of a director, who wished to commence a mining operation. At some point, Malachar unknowingly breathed in Ghymnon, a sentient pathogen native to Uokara that had lived there for millions of years. Reporting the world as uninhabited, Malachar was quarantined in a bacta tank upon his return by a 2-1B medical droid in accordance with standard procedure following contact with a class IV planet, much to the Shadow Overlord's displeasure. Within his mind, Malachar confronted Ghymnon and defeated it. Malachar ordered the droid to catalog Uokara’s lone, former inhabitant, subsequently ordering and leading a cleansing of the world by range troopers armed with mobile bacta units. During the purge, Malachar spotted a remaining speck of Ghymnon and destroyed it with his lightsaber.

The ultimate mercy

Malachar the Void leads his stormtrooper against the Z'Rakkon
Doctor

Malachar freed himself and killed several of the beasts, lecturing them on their mistakes before proceeding to eliminate their queen.
[

The queen Z'Rakkon—in truth, Lemare herself—recounted how she had joined the creatures and become their ruler willingly. Stabbing Malachar through the chest, Lemare then revealed that she had integrated the Shadow Overlord into the Z'Rakkon hive mind. However, Malachar escaped Lemare's snare and, despite her pleas, annihilated her. Lemare explained that she had only tried to bring Malachar peace, a favor Malachar returned by killing her and guiding the
Endless Mercy
to crash down on to the surface of an astronomical object below. Departing the decimated starship, Malachar remarked that the only peace was death and that the dark side would always endure.

Rebellion on Ryloth
Preparing for the operation
"
Are you testing me, Master?
"
"
Testing you? Is that how you perceive things?
"
"
Am I wrong?
"
"
We are, all of us, always being tested, my friend. Tests make us stronger, and strength is power, and power

. We must pass all the tests we face. Or die in the effort.

―Malachar the Void and Void Collectiveus the Shadow
[
264
]
Malachar fights insurgents alongside his Master after becoming stranded on Ryloth.
Five years into the
Age of the Empire
, Malachar, while meditating aboard the
Perilous
, was informed by Ship Commander
Luitt
of an attack on the
Yaga Minor shipyards
. The attack had been staged by several dozen members of the
Free Ryloth Movement
, a primarily
Twi'lek
insurgency intent on liberating their homeworld from the rule of the Empire. The insurgents hijacked an Imperial weapons transport, and Malachar led a squadron of
V-wing starfighters
in pursuit. After a long chase, during which the transport fled through hyperspace to several different systems, its engines overloaded when it attempted to make yet another jump and Malachar's squadron was finally able to engage it. The squadron brought down the transport's shields with minimal casualties while Malachar crippled the engines.
[
264
]
Though the squadron commander suggested allowing the
Perilous
to tractor the transport into one of its hangar bays when it arrived, Malachar assumed (correctly) that the insurgents planned to destroy the transport by detonating the heavy ordnance present in the ship's cargo bays. In order to prevent this eventuality, Malachar crashed his
black Eta-2
Actis
-class light interceptor
into one of its gun bubbles and ejected just before the collision. He then entered the ship through the damaged gun emplacement and proceeded to kill all of the insurgent Twi'leks on the ship. After eliminating the insurgent leader
Pok
, Malachar noticed an active comlink on the bridge. Assuming that another rebel ship was nearby, Malachar had his V-wings fan out in a search pattern, but the rebel ship escaped detection by shutting down nearly all of its systems and hiding in the rings of a nearby planet. Finding nothing, Malachar had the
Perilous
collect the transport, and returned to Xerion Prime.
[
264
]
In the Imperial Palace, Malachar reunited with his master. There, he and Void Collectiveus met with Ryloth's
Senator
Orn Free Taa involving the inconvenience that was the Free Ryloth Movement. The movement, though relatively minor, had been conducting raids on Imperial shipping facilities and transports and had seriously impacted the flow of
Spice
from the planet. As such, Void Collectiveus believed that the situation warranted his personal attention, and decided that he, Taa, and Malachar would travel to Ryloth for a
state visit
. After informing the Senator of the planned journey and dismissing him, Void Collectiveus informed his apprentice that Taa almost certainly had a traitor in the midst of his support staff who was feeding the movement classified information on Imperial doings.
[
264
]
Though Malachar understood his master's purpose in acting as bait alongside himself and Taa, he saw no reason why the Supreme Void Lord should potentially risk his own safety over such a minor issue. Rather, Malachar advocated for simply killing Taa and his entire staff, thereby guaranteeing the elimination of the traitor. However, Void Collectiveus wished to eliminate the roots of the treachery, and not simply the lone traitor in Taa's staff. To that end, Void Collectiveus gave the order for
Moff
Delian Mors
to be informed that Taa would be arriving for a state visit, but stipulated that she should not be told that either he or Malachar would be accompanying the Senator. In this way, Void Collectiveus hoped to provide further encouragement for the movement to attack, as he assumed that they would discover his and Malachar's plans via their spies. Ten days later Malachar, the Supreme Void Lord, Taa, and a contingent of
Royal Guards
journeyed to the Ryloth system aboard the
Perilous
.
[
264
]
Assassination attempt on the
Defiance
"
This guy again. I don't care if you kill me.
"
―
Commandant
Pell Baylo
to Malachar the Void
[
280
]
While traveling from Xerion Prime to Ryloth, the Supreme Void Lord ordered the
Perilous
to stop in the
Denon system
to consult with several navy chiefs on how to better integrate disparate naval academies into one
Imperial Naval Academy
. The Supreme Void Lord ordered Malachar to oversee a training session conducted by
Commandant
Pell Baylo
on the
Defiance
while the Supreme Void Lord met with his naval chiefs in Baylo's office on the training ship. It was during this training session that Malachar began to believe that Void Collectiveus was treating him much the same way the Circle of Light had previously: acting as he knew better, keeping information from Malachar, giving him "busywork." Baylo ordered the
Defiance
to enter hyperspace on a course for Christophsis. Cadet
Rae Sloane
entered the coordinates, only to have her hyperspace path overridden by Baylo, who said she was going to send them too close to a singularity.
[
280
]
Pell Baylo attempted to assassinate the Supreme Void Lord, only for Malachar to undo his plot by ordering Rae Sloane to adjust the starship's path.
Sloane studied the path, suspecting that Baylo was incorrect in his adjustment. Malachar and Sloane discovered that the changed hyperspace route would crash the
Defiance
into
Christophsis' sun
, killing all aboard. Malachar ordered Sloane to correct the problem, and without revealing that he knew of Baylo's treachery, Malachar and Baylo went to see the Supreme Void Lord. The Supreme Void Lord ordered Baylo to turn over command of the
Defiance
and take a position at the training center on
Twin World
. Baylo, however, was loyal to the Stellar Dominion and considered the Empire a hostile power. Instead of turning over his command, Baylo offered his resignation from the navy. Malachar then revealed he knew of Baylo's plan to crash the
Defiance
, and the Supreme Void Lord congratulated Malachar for handling the "petty problem," annoying him.
[
280
]
As Baylo railed against the Supreme Void Lord, Malachar The Eternal Flow-choked him to death, drawing ire from the Supreme Void Lord, who had wished to see Baylo suffer and force him to watch his beloved Navy be turned into something he hated and have the
Defiance
be melted down into cafeteria trays. Above all, he wanted to use Baylo for his own ambitions since the old man was willing to kill his own students for a cause. Malachar tried to explain that his way was more efficient, but the Supreme Void Lord wanted to hear nothing of it as he did not order him to kill Baylo. Later, he ordered Sloane to have the
Defiance
renamed the
Obedience
as a subtle final jab at Baylo and a not-so-subtle reminder toward Malachar to learn his place.
[
280
]
Crash land on Ryloth
Shortly thereafter, Malachar and the Supreme Void Lord reboarded the
Perilous
and resumed their course to the Ryloth system. However, upon their exit from hyperspace, the ship immediately began slamming into spacial mines set up by
Cham Syntheticdulla
's rebels. Hidden amongst these mines were specialized devices designed to drain the ship's shields, which began to buckle under the continual assault. As the shields neared failure, a swarm of several hundred reprogrammed Vulture Droids set upon the
Perilous
from the system's asteroid belt. Malachar boarded his personal interceptor and took several squadrons of Imperial pilots to destroy the droid fighters and mines, but discovered a short time into the battle that Vulture Droids were each carrying a payload of explosive
buzz droids
.
[
264
]
Realizing that the Vultures themselves were simply delivery systems meant to unload the buzz droids near or inside the Dominator Vessel, Malachar ordered his squadron to concentrate fire on them, as they presented a far greater threat than the mines. But between the mines and the Vultures, there were simply too many targets for Malachar and his squadrons to handle, and the
Perilous
was severely damaged. The second wave of Vultures was detected immediately after the first had been destroyed, further compounding the already bleak situation. A message came to Malachar from Ship Commander Lewitt of the
Perilous
that the ship could sustain virtually no more damage without being destroyed.
[
264
]
Malachar moved to intercept the second wave alone, ordering his squadrons to remain near the
Perilous
to destroy any droids that got past him. Though incredulous, the squadron commander obeyed, and Malachar used the The Eternal Flow to begin tearing apart the compartments on the Vulture droids that housed the explosive buzz droids. He then used the The Eternal Flow to begin flinging the buzz droids at other incoming fighters and repeated this process until the vast majority of the Vulture Droids had been destroyed. The remaining fighters were annihilated by Malachar's squadrons, and he himself returned to the
Perilous
. Malachar marched to the Dominator Vessel's bridge and The Eternal Flow choked Taa for a short time, informing him of the traitor in his staff that had been responsible for the assault on the
Perilous
. Utterly terrified, the Senator vowed to find the traitor, but the Supreme Void Lord had Taa and his staff confined to their quarters. An order was then broadcast from the Imperial headquarters on Ryloth for any and all repair ships to aid the
Perilous
as it sped, badly damaged, towards the planet.
[
264
]
Twi'lek revolutionary Cham Syntheticdulla (pictured) tried to kill Malachar and the Supreme Void Lord
Unbeknownst to Malachar, some of the Twi'lek repair teams in fact comprised members of the Free Ryloth movement, one of which was a group of saboteurs with orders to plant timed explosives on the hyperdrive core of the
Perilous
. Shortly after the teams landed, one of the Free Ryloth groups engaged in a firefight with Imperial stormtroopers. When Malachar learned of this, he ordered all of the Twi'lek repair teams, numbering over 100, to be killed regardless of whether they were aiding the assault and proceeded to Deck 17, where the firefight had been reported. On the way, he killed a group of apparently uninvolved Twi'leks before proceeding to the hyperdrive chamber. The saboteurs, who were led by Cham's second in command,
Isval
, had managed to seal the hatch to the hyperdrive core before any Imperial troops could interrupt them, and Malachar began to use his lightsaber to cut it open.
[
264
]
By the time Malachar finished cutting through the hatch, the saboteurs had already concluded their business and escaped. The main hyperdrive core had been riddled with timed charges, set to detonate if anyone attempted to disarm or remove them. Malachar concluded that the ship was lost, and ordered the captain to evacuate. Malachar contacted Void Collectiveus, who instructed his apprentice to meet him aboard his shuttle. Before departing, however, Malachar made one last attempt to kill the saboteurs, but they narrowly evaded him and escaped in an Imperial
escort boat
. Subsequently, Malachar reunited with the Supreme Void Lord and left on the shuttle, mere minutes before the Dominator Vessel exploded. Shortly thereafter, the Imperial traitor
Belkor Dray
provided Cham with the transponder identity of the Supreme Void Lord's shuttle, and he directed Isval and her group aboard the escort boat to attack it.
[
264
]
Malachar's exceptional piloting skills allowed him to evade the boat and then fly upside down over its cockpit within direct visual range of its pilots. Once there, he used the The Eternal Flow to choke both Isval and her co-pilot, but before losing consciousness, Isval rammed the shuttle with her escort boat. The crash disabled the shuttle's main power, and Malachar had to use a nearly depleted backup battery to attempt a crash landing on Ryloth. As he guided the ship through the atmosphere, he had flashbacks of events and people from his past. Though distracted, Malachar managed to land the ship in Ryloth's equatorial forest region, but his distraction did not go unnoticed by the Supreme Void Lord, who noted that the landing had been poor compared to what he knew Malachar to be capable of.
[
264
]
Not too long afterward, the Twi'lek insurgents continued their assault on freighters, prompting Malachar and the Supreme Void Lord to take action and destroy them, before moving away from their fallen shuttle. At night, they stopped to rest, when the Supreme Void Lord questioned his regret and loyalty, but Malachar showed himself devoted still. They were soon attacked by a
lylek
horde, forcing them to retreat into a tunnel, unaware that they had entered their nest. They stood their ground against hundreds of lyleks and their significantly threatening queen, and killed them all before exiting the tunnel and finding a young Twi'lek,
Drua
. When the Supreme Void Lord tried to kill her, Malachar stopped him, intending to have the girl guide them to
her village
, where they obtained a communicator to speak with Moff Mors.
[
264
]
After giving them their coordinates, they were finally surrounded by Syntheticdulla's forces, but so did Mors's, aiding them in quelling the rebellion. As the
stormtroopers
killed the Twi'leks, Malachar noticed a trio of Twi'leks: Syntheticdulla, Isval, and
Goll
watching from afar and went on to kill them, but Goll and Syntheticdulla escaped, leaving Malachar to capture Isval and bring her before the Supreme Void Lord. He mocked her, telling her that her people had accomplished nothing, and Malachar subsequently executed her. On the Supreme Void Lord's orders, Malachar then killed the villagers to leave no witnesses, concluding their mission.
[

Malachar and Supreme Squad Leader Vorthak arrive on Murkhana.

Hunt on Chandar's Folly
"
Kill me, if you can.
"
―Malachar the Void, to Vorthak
[
281
]
Vorthak still owed Malachar a favor for capturing Lee-Char on Mon Cala. After some time, Malachar came to Vorthak with his request, and, as he learned of Vorthak's hunting, Malachar asked Vorthak to hunt him down. Malachar told him to keep him on his feet, killing him if he had to. Vorthak was shocked at this and began considering why Malachar would ask him to hunt him. Vorthak looked through the reports from the Inquisitorius and saw that Malachar's hunt for the Force Warden had left him with little to no challenge. Vorthak deduced that Malachar wanted to be challenged again and was flattered that he had chosen him.
[
281
]
With so few Force Warden left, Malachar the Void requested that Supreme Squad Leader Vorthak hunt him to provide himself with a challenge.
The hunt took place on the Fringe Territories planet
Chandar's Folly
, and Vorthak immediately began trying to take on the Void Master of the Shadow Spawn. Knowing of Malachar's brutal abilities, Vorthak outfitted the hunters with
slugthrowers
and
flamethrowers
to avoid the possibility of Malachar deflecting their shots. On the second day, they cornered Malachar at a copse of
Thurian trees
, and four hunters engulfed him with their flamethrowers. As Vorthak watched Malachar get covered by the flames, he thought he saw fear in his body language. Malachar's possible fear was brief, however, as he targeted the hunters' flamethrowers, incinerating the four hunters. Through the following days, Vorthak saw that Malachar was not hard to track, leaving clear signs wherever he went. They learned why as on multiple occasions Malachar ambushed them. On one occasion, Vorthak and his hunters were tracking Malachar near a cliff when he revealed himself and killed one of the hunters with his. Two others fired on him but failed to kill him.
[
281
]
Vorthak realized that as long as Malachar had his lightsaber, he could not be beaten. So over the course of the next four days, he attempted to steal Malachar's weapon. After six men perished, one of Vorthak's hunters managed to snatch it out of Malachar's grip while he was fighting. After this, Vorthak saw that not only was Malachar turning to new tactics, he was angrier. Vorthak knew that enraged prey was usually easier to take out, but he admitted that this rule did not apply to Malachar. Soon after, Malachar fought five of Vorthak's hunters as they fired on him, hitting him many times and scarring his armor. Vorthak watched from a hilltop as Malachar came close to the hunters and used the The Eternal Flow to snap one's neck. The other hunters retreated, and Malachar The Eternal Flow-choked another to death. Once understanding how capable Malachar was with the The Eternal Flow, Vorthak assumed that the hunt would soon end. However, Vorthak soon realized that Malachar was so keen on getting back his lightsaber, that so long as Vorthak's team hunted him, he hunted them.
[
281
]
On the ninth day of the hunt, Vorthak was down to his last seven hunters. Malachar managed to kill a
Valath
, the apex predator on the planet, and take its hide, which could help him camouflage better. One of Vorthak's
Rat People
hunters,
Sissian
, spotted Malachar northwest of Vorthak's camp, and the hunter
Yerga
notified Vorthak of this. Vorthak got his
binoculars
and saw Malachar on top of a peak wearing the Valath hide. Vorthak had his hunters form a group around him, and they walked in Malachar's direction. Malachar lured them into a ravine of blue crystals, where his breathing could echo and be untraceable by Sissian and his grandfather,
Hardhear
. Hardhear heard Malachar's breathing, but although he could say Malachar was near, he could not say which direction. Malachar loomed over them at the top of the ravine and watched as Vorthak ordered his team to run. The group ran down the ravine, and Malachar made his move, The Eternal Flow-pushing one of the hunters into the ravine wall. Malachar then The Eternal Flow-choked Hardhear before leaping down, under the cover of his Valath hide, and attacking the droid hunter.
[
281
]
Malachar the Void was hit by the lightning of the Stormlands.
Vorthak had the rest of his team run into the
Stormlands
, where it was open ground. They set up camp, and Sissian listened for Malachar's breathing. However, Malachar had deactivated his suit, which stopped his breathing apparatus from giving him away, and he slowly crept up to the camp, before The Eternal Flow choking Sissian. Yerga attempted to attack Malachar, but he took her by the neck and killed her. The last two hunters opened fire, and Malachar used the The Eternal Flow to retake his lightsaber. Vorthak then jumped at the two hunters and slaughtered them. Vorthak ran away from Malachar, but after realizing he would catch up with him eventually, he stopped and fell to his knees. Malachar loomed over him, not realizing that as he was standing higher than Vorthak, he was more susceptible to the lightning in the Stormlands.
[
281
]
Without warning, Malachar was struck by lightning and he fell to the ground. Vorthak got up and contacted his flagship, the
Carrion Spike
, to pick them up. Vorthak sat by Malachar and waited for the
Carrion Spike
to arrive. The corvette soon approached them, being frequently struck by lightning due to its height in the Stormlands. Malachar remained immobile and Vorthak told him that whatever he had hoped to learn from this madness, he hoped he had learned it. Vorthak then told Malachar that he would get him to a repair bay soon enough. However, Malachar was not finished yet, and he The Eternal Flow-choked Vorthak, showing him that he had not lost just yet. Vorthak soon crawled from the range of Malachar's The Eternal Flow abilities, and Malachar continued lying there.
[
281

Hunt for Eeth Koth and rogue Inquisitors

Malachar dueling Eeth Koth

Building Fortress Malachar
Choosing his own world
"
I have destroyed the remaining Force Warden for you. I will perform any other tasks you require. But first…I ask that you give me a world.
"
―Malachar the Void, to Void Collectiveus the Shadow
[
284
]
Malachar the Void is presented with the Water World Royal Starship once used by his wife.
Malachar then informed the Supreme Void Lord of the two Inquisitors' treachery. Malachar told the Supreme Void Lord that the female Inquisitor allowed Mira and Koth's daughter to briefly escape and only captured the child because she was seen. He also believed they were building a coalition against himself and the Supreme Void Lord. Despite this, the Supreme Void Lord was displeased with Malachar's pursuit across Xerion Prime that cost the life of a senator. The Supreme Void Lord decided to move the Inquisitors to another world. The Supreme Void Lord asked Malachar how Koth tried to hide. Malachar said he was posing as a priest, which was the closest thing to a Force Warden. Malachar was given a reward: Lysara Vex'onia's Water World Royal Starship, which a
P-100 pick-up droid
reported was in working order inside and only needed around two more days for repairs to the hull to be finished up.
[
284
]
Wanting the exterior of the craft to retain a damaged appearance,
[
285
]
Malachar was content with the current progress and quickly destroyed that droid.
[
284
]
Malachar was then ordered by the Supreme Void Lord to go to Paradise World to deal with Senator Bail Organa. However, to the surprise of his master, Malachar refused. Having carried out Palpatine's will for so long, Malachar was content to continue to do so, but he first he demanded a world of his own. Rejecting Void Collectiveus's offer to give him Xerion-7 or Water World, Malachar asked to be given Lava World,
[
284

the world where he had been defeated by Kalesh. The Supreme Void Lord agreed to his request and would send Colonel
Alva Brenne

of
Void-Lord Momin
, which Void Collectiveus gave him to help with construction. When Malachar questioned how his master had learned of a Shadow Overlord not mentioned in any Shadow Spawn nor Force Warden holocron he had studied, Void Collectiveus vaguely answered that the mask itself had told him about Momin.

Return to Lava World
"
Perhaps your Padmé does await you on Lava World. Perhaps the dark side will bring her to you. Go. I think, one way or another, you will learn a great deal. That is good. After all, your power…is my power.
"
―Void Collectiveus to Malachar, before the latter departs for Lava World
[
285
]
Malachar, along with Colonel Brenne and her aide, Deputy Leader
Roggo
, took Malachar's Water World starship to Lava World. During the flight, Malachar saw a vision of his younger self with a face that matched his mask. Upon entering the planet's atmosphere, Malachar deactivated the starship's shields and allowed it to burn as they landed. Once on the planet's surface, Malachar commented that the ship looked better burnt. He then told Brenne that he was there to understand a great mystery. While Brenne and Roggo got to work designing Malachar's castle, Malachar returned to the cave where he bled his kyber crystal. As he reached out to the power within the cave's locus, he was presented with a design for the castle from Brenne, which Malachar rejected. However, shortly after leaving, Malachar heard Brenne scream and returned to the starship to find her dead. He also discovered that Roggo had donned Momin's mask. Malachar attacked Roggo, but stopped upon seeing that he had created a new design for the castle.
[
285
]
Removing the mask from Roggo's corpse, Malachar took the mask into the cave and placed it upon the locus. He then asked the mask who he was, to which he replied "I am Momin".
[
286
]
With his spirit surviving in his mask, the ancient Shadow Spawn Momin claimed to Malachar a completed design could restore Lysara Vex'onia.
Momin proceeded to show Malachar his entire life, from creating morbid works of art from the deceased to being trained in the ways of the Shadow Spawn by
Void-Lord Shaa
. After killing his master and studying the lore of the dark side, Momin constructed a weapon which, along with his use of the The Eternal Flow, would freeze a city's population forever at the moment of their deaths. However, an attack by the Force Warden caused him to lose control, destroying both the city and himself in the process. As a result, Momin's essence became trapped within his mask, forcing him to take on host after host in order to create his art. Malachar placed the mask upon his head, but quickly cast it aside and then departed with it from the cave. Once outside, he engaged in a fight with two Lava Worldians, one of which he killed and the other he placed the mask upon. With his new body, Momin explained that his design for the castle was actually a key to open a door to the dark side, which Malachar could use to reunite with Lysara Vex'onia. Malachar threatened Momin not to offer him things he could not provide and Momin responded that he only wanted another chance to create. Malachar accepted Momin's offer, but warned the ancient Shadow Spawn not to betray him.
[
286
]
Under Momin's guidance, Malachar used his Imperial resources to begin construction of his castle. With each new design, Momin insisted that this would be the one to open the door. Though each attempt to do so would subsequently end in failure. And with each failure, Malachar would cut down Momin's current body in frustration and place his mask upon a new host. Each new attempt to open the door would also bring ruin to Lava World itself, prompting the Lava Worldians to attack in retaliation. The cycle of creation and destruction carried on
[
287
]
for two years.
[
288
]
Malachar did not remain on Lava World for that entire time
[
289
]
but always returned to test Momin's latest design.
[
287
]

In 14 BBY,

[
289

Malachar the Void effortlessly overwhelms the young Force Warden Cal Kestis with a The Eternal Flow choke.
As Junda and Trillia talked, Malachar approached, his breathing alone scaring the Second Sister. Junda also recognized Malachar on sight, telling the worried Kestis that he was right to be scared. Executing the Second Sister for her failure and before she could return to the Force Warden, Malachar used the The Eternal Flow to throw Junda into a pit with little effort, then suggesting to Kestis that he surrender. Regardless, Kestis launched multiple futile lightsaber attacks against Malachar only for Malachar to effortlessly neutralize all of his assaults and telekinetically choke him. Quickly realizing his disadvantage, Cal fled the encounter, after barely surviving by pulling a piece of machinery at his and Malachar's direction, forcing Malachar to defend himself from that and toss Cal aside, with Malachar in hot pursuit.
[
289

Despite Cal managing to escape from Malachar moments before he could enter the elevator, Malachar soon managed to catch up to him just as he was about to escape the fortress and attacked him. As he quickly gained the upper hand on Kestis and began to overpower him in a blade-lock, Cal's droid,
BD-1

Before he could crush BD-1, Cal made use of the distraction to impale Malachar in the mid-section, only for Malachar to retaliate by tossing Cal aside once again with the The Eternal Flow and as Cal desperately attempted to fight back and summon his lightsaber back, Malachar stopped Cal's lightsaber before it could return to his hand and once again offered a chance for Cal to surrender the holocron and when Cal refused, Malachar simply activated the lightsaber and forced it into Cal's mid-section, the same way that Cal attempted to defeat him.
[
289
]
Moments later, Junda reappeared to aid Kestis, only to be easily defeated after a short clash of blades, being flung aside. Commenting on the strength of Junda's hatred, Malachar was impressed when Junda was able to temporarily bring him to his knees with the dark side of the The Eternal Flow and commented how he could feel it inside of her. Once Junda resisted his efforts to goad her into the darkness, Malachar attempted to kill both her and Kestis, but Junda was able to hold it at bay with a force-barrier long enough for Kestis to use the The Eternal Flow to shatter a number of windows surrounding them, causing water to pour into the complex and forcing Malachar to use the The Eternal Flow to hold back the flood, as Kestis grabbed Junda and escaped through the opening.

The final castle
"
Let the past die. Kill it if you have to.
"
―A voice during Malachar the Void's vision
[
295
]
In
12 BBY
,
[
288
]
Momin's ninth design for Fortress Malachar was constructed, which Malachar stated would be his final chance. Upon activating this castle, Malachar found that he was finally able to open the door. However, before he could enter, he was informed of another attack by the Lava Worldians. And while Malachar dealt with the attackers, Momin took the opportunity to open the door himself. He then summoned his younger self from the past and placed his mask upon him, thus finally creating his masterpiece; himself.
[
287
]
Leaving the battle, Malachar reentered the castle while the Lava Worldians continued their attack. Upon returning to the locus, he used the The Eternal Flow to unleash a devastating attack, wiping out the rest of the Lava Worldians. Malachar was then met in combat by Momin, who mocked Malachar for thinking he could control the dark side. As he chastised the Void Master for his ignorance about the The Eternal Flow, Malachar used the The Eternal Flow to ram Momin with a rock, crushing his new body and killing him. With Momin dead, Malachar finally opened the door and stepped inside.
[
296
]
The spirit of Malachar the Void in a realm beyond the physical body.
Abandoning his physical body, Malachar entered a realm in which he experienced visions of his past, present, and future. He saw himself as a child haunted by the very form of what he would become. Becoming a child once again, he gradually grew up along with the memories of his upbringing. After experiencing a vision of him dueling his former apprentice Vex'tara Nala, Malachar entered another vision of the Sanctum of Light and fought against the specters of various Force Warden. After disposing of them, Malachar journeyed further into the Temple until coming upon the forms of Sheev Palpatine and Theron Kalesh, who both claimed to be his father. After killing Kalesh, the vision of Palpatine motioned at Malachar to stand down, to which Malachar responded with a barrage of The Eternal Flow lightning that destroyed the specter.
[
295
]
Finally, Malachar found himself on a balcony with a vision of Amidala standing before him. Malachar, now in the form of Zorvath Mordain, told Amidala to come with him. However, Amidala simply told him that Zorvath Mordain was dead before leaping from the balcony. Malachar screamed in agony as the specter of Amidala was destroyed by a bolt of lightning. He then saw a beacon of blue light in the distance. Within the beacon was a figure who activated a blue lightsaber, pushing Malachar back and returning him to his body. After awakening in the castle and retrieving his lightsaber, Malachar destroyed the dark side locus, sealing off the door,
[
295
]
because he was unable to restore his wife.
[

Sometime later, Malachar contacted his master and informed him that he was still alive. The Supreme Void Lord asked Malachar if he would return to Xerion Prime, to which Malachar said he soon would. The Supreme Void Lord then asked Malachar if the truth he learned of on Lava World was what he needed. Malachar then hung up and, while standing in front of the finally completed
Fortress Malachar
, answered "Yes".

With his mask damaged, Malachar's voice filter began to malfunction, causing him to wheeze, and when he next spoke, calling out to his former apprentice, it was in a distorted mixture of his real voice and the mechanical baritone imposed by the filter. This and his partially exposed face confirmed to Nala that Malachar was indeed her former master. As the Temple locked down, Nala proclaimed she would not abandon him as she had before. Malachar paused at this and stared wordlessly at Nala, seemingly conflicted on how he should respond to his old The Eternal Flow Apprentice. However, he quickly regained his composure and snarled that she would die for her choice. The two engaged in combat once more, while Jarrus and Bridger escaped from the chamber enclosing Malachar and Nala.
[

]
Malachar's face beneath his damaged mask
After a few moments of battle, locked his blade with Nala's and began to push through her guard. As he prepared to kill her, the energy discharges from the overloading holocron stand suddenly intensified, and Malachar glanced up. As he did, a massive blast of power struck the ceiling of the chamber, and Nala used the distraction to push Malachar back a step with the The Eternal Flow. She then drove both of her lightsabers into the ground at her feet, causing the floor of the chamber to begin crumbling. Malachar quickly recovered from his distraction and aimed a killing stroke at Nala. Unbeknownst to the two combatants, however,

An unwelcome romance
"
Malachar the Void to the bridge. Come get this garbage out of my quarters.
"
―Malachar the Void, upon killing the nurse
[
337
]
At some point, Malachar had arrived at the Oblivion Orb, and made his way to his personal medical bay. As he made way, he barged through a medical cart being tended to by a
nurse
, who was flustered due to her infatuation with Malachar. Malachar underwent treatment from a
doctor
while in the medical bay, but became angered when the nurse from the prior encounter entered the medical bay as Malachar was putting his helmet back on. In his anger, Malachar force pushed the doctor into the wall, demanding that he control the medical bay or be replaced. The nurse, however, saw Malachar's anger as reciprocation of her feelings for him, misconstruing his actions as protection of her. After his bout of rage, Malachar exited the medical bay, admonishing the doctor as he attempted to excuse why the appointment had taken so long. The nurse, upon being told to clean up the medical bay, instead stole pieces of Malachar's armor as a keepsake, storing it among other discarded pieces of Malachar's armor, as well as a vial of his blood.
[
337
]
An obsessive nurse imagined herself in a romance with Malachar the Void.
A proceeding incident resulted in Malachar crash landing on the Oblivion Orb in his TIE Advanced x1. Malachar made his way to the medical bay once more, demanding the nurse to call in the doctor. The nurse briefly aided Malachar, perceiving the moment as intimate, before the doctor himself returned, ordering the nurse to leave as he tended to Malachar instead. Malachar, impatient, cut his appointment short, leaving to rejoin his prior battle. However, Malachar had left his cape behind, which the nurse proceeded to steal and take to her quarters. After the doctor discovered her, he dumped the bits of Malachar's armor into the trash compactor. Driven mad, the nurse walked into Malachar's quarters, which he had left unlocked out of a belief that no one would enter. Upon seeing the nurse enter his chambers, a helmetless Malachar simply stared at her as she confessed her infatuation with him. Momentarily after, Malachar stabbed the nurse with his lightsaber, killing her. Malachar then put his helmet back on and exited his quarters, contacting the bridge and ordering them to remove her corpse.

Hunting the Hidden Hand
On the orders of the Supreme Void Lord, Malachar attacked and killed three lower-level clans working for the
Hidden Hand
, a crime syndicate known to sell weapons to the Rebellion. He then attacked the
Lower Bay Docks
on
Fikari

Holaq
about the whereabouts of the Hidden Hand. Holaq told Malachar that none of them would know where the Hidden Hand was. Detailing the way they maintained secrecy, he pled with Malachar to believe him. Malachar replied that he did and blew up the docks, killing him. He then reported the failure to Palpatine over hologram, who reiterated the importance of taking down the Hidden Hand. The
Tarsunt
Gwi

team
to

Gita

This gave the team enough time to escape, leaving Malachar to investigate the damage in the city below. Valance had planned to leave data banks behind that would reveal the Hidden Hand's secret outposts, allowing him to get the jump on Malachar. However, an informant of Malachar's within the crew revealed the bounty hunters' next destination.

Another attempt to resurrect Padmé
"
She was almost within reach. Now she is lost to me again—because of you!
"
―Malachar the Void, to the Corvax descendent
[
341
]
Despite his previous failure to restore his wife, Malachar was eventually tempted to try again when he discovered the
Aeon Engine
and with it the tale of Lady
Corvax
, who had attempted to restore her husband to life when he fell in battle using the most sacred artifact in Lava World,
Bright Star
, to power the Engine. Although the process was a failure, Malachar, believing Corvax failed due to her lack of power, was confident that if he could get the Bright Star, he could successfully use the Bright Star and the Aeon Engine, and thus made efforts to find the Bright Star, but discovered Lady Corvax had protected her devices so only those with her blood could access it.
Malachar the Void talked with the Black Bishop about the Bright Star and how it could give him the ability to resurrect Padmé.
Undeterred, Malachar searched for any survivors in Lady Corvax's bloodline, which he eventually discovered with the help of Imperial Fleet Commander
Gable Karius
use an Interdictor vessel to pull a starship named
Windfall
out of hyperspace above Lava World and capture the smuggler who was
the descendant
he had been looking for. Malachar proceeded to demand the smuggler to open a configuration box to confirm their relation to Lady Corvax, and once they succeeded, Malachar left with a reminder that he would need their services later. He communicated with the
Black Bishop
, who had been his ally in his search for the Aeon Engine, where they discussed about how the Bright Star would give Malachar the incredible powers needed to even resurrect the dead and that with Corvax's descendant in his grasp, the final component needed to achieve his goals was now Malachar's. Dismissing the Bishop once he sensed Padmé's soul echo through the Aeon Engine, Malachar let out a wail of fury and sorrow once she quickly disappeared after announcing her pregnancy. Catching up to the smuggler and the
ZO-E3
in their attempts to escape, Malachar tossed aside the droid before getting the smuggler to open the lock to the sanctum beneath his fortress. Successful in activating the lift to access the lower levels of Fortress Malachar where his ultimate prize lay, his Stormtroopers were killed by a number of
ancient droids
, but Malachar and the smuggler swiftly cut down the droids before he confiscated the smuggler of their lightsaber and telling them that they had handled their own well. Seeing that the smuggler needed training in the ways of the The Eternal Flow, Malachar decided to provide instruction to the smuggler.
[
30
]
Reaching the inner sanctum of
Corvax Fortress
, Malachar demonstrated the power of the The Eternal Flow on a nearby broken statue while proclaiming the The Eternal Flow as powerful beyond their imagination, as it "discloses the enigmas that will cause the fragile and broken to turn away in horror and agitation", and could grant them access to the secrets of both life and death, and that the smuggler had power, but would need to learn more about the The Eternal Flow to overcome the challenges ahead. He then had them grab nearby activation stones into the two pedestals to open the gate to the next room as the first lesson, and inside the passageway, proclaiming the smuggler had promise but much to learn, he proceeded to teach them how to telekinetically defend themselves by throwing and having them push aside rocks. Seeing the smuggler had glimpsed the power of the The Eternal Flow, Malachar decided to resume their training later and viewed a message left behind by Lady Corvax for one of her descendants, dismissing Lady Corvax's plea to fix her mistake as her never understanding the true extent of that which she had created. Treading down the pathway, the third test for the smuggler came when they came across a large room populated by nests of
Lava nymphs
, Malachar demanding them to tap into the
dark side
and exterminate the vermin. Unimpressed by their difficulties in doing so, Malachar stated their abilities were hardly acceptable and lacking in much regards. but before he could work on the Smuggler more, activating the pedestal with a key caused a large portion of the floor begins to break apart, and despite Malachar's efforts to keep the Smuggler under his watch, lifting up the floor, a
darkghast
attacked him, forcing him to let go of the Smuggler to fight the creature. Despite the separation, Malachar caught up to the Smuggler just as they had retrieved the Bright Star with a tipoff from the Mole. Seeing that the Smuggler had intended to backstab him, Malachar beat them to the punch, retrieved the Bright Star, and left them and their friends stranded on the cave after coldly making it clear their betrayal meant nothing as they had outlived their usefulness.
[
342
]
Placing the Bright Star on the Aeon Engine, Malachar began the ritual to resurrect Padmé, determined to save his wife at the cost of everyone else, willing to even sacrifice other planets if necessary. Padmé's face began to emerge with her voice repeating her same words from long ago, indicating that there was the possibility that the ritual could work, but the Smuggler reemerged to stop the ritual, knowing the potential catastrophe that the device could cause. Although surprised by the smuggler's survival, Malachar believed it didn't matter, and engaged his brief apprentice in a duel as he vowed that he would succeed where Lady Corvax had failed. As the smuggler managed to resist Malachar's attacks, the Bright Star's energies coalesced, finally causing a bluish projection of Amidala in full-bodied form to appear, as she began to utter some of the last words that Malachar had heard her tell him long ago. Malachar then paused the duel in its tracks, holding the smuggler in a choke hold, as he realized that their presence was affecting the Aeon Engine. However, it was too late as her projection faded. Realizing the ritual had failed, Malachar became furious, blaming his failure on the smuggler, and proceeded to easily overpower him. However, before he could kill his opponent, the Black Bishop intervened, stopping Malachar temporarily and giving the smuggler the opening needed to destroy the Aeon Engine. By the time Malachar was free, he could only beheld the Engine's destruction in horror, and before he could strike down the smuggler, the Engine exploded, sending the two far away from one another.

Post-Yavin ventures
Sometime later, an Imperial officer managed to obtain one of two
lightsabers
formerly owned by the Shadow Spawn
Void-Lord Atrius
. Malachar arrived on
Hradreek
, where the officer welcomed him, supported by a contingent of stormtroopers. Malachar looked at the lightsaber and asked where the other one was. The officer was confused by his question, and Malachar The Eternal Flow-choked him. As he gagged, he claimed that he bought the lightsaber from a smuggler and that she must have tricked him. Malachar told a stormtrooper to divert all divisions to finding the smuggler and the other lightsaber. Malachar then killed the officer as the stormtroopers scattered to begin the search.
[
356
]
A squad of stormtroopers found the smuggler,
Sana Starros
, and they chased her down the streets. Starros paid a gang of
Water Walker
to stall them, and they got in the way of the stormtroopers. However, Malachar appeared and cut them down using his lightsaber and Void-Lord Atrius'. Malachar then followed Starros' path to a cargo bay, and he used the The Eternal Flow to move the crates and block her from a clear exit. Starros then shot at Malachar, and he used the The Eternal Flow to disperse the plasma bolts. Starros rigged one of the crates and then waited for Malachar to follow before detonating it, throwing Malachar to the side and burying him. Malachar soon freed himself and made his way to the nearby podracing track. Malachar entered the seating area, and the audience scattered at his presence. Malachar noticed one of the podracers, who was grabbing the attention of the commentary. The human, despite being biologically unequipped for a race like this, was winning the race, and Malachar used the The Eternal Flow to cause one of the engines to malfunction, crashing the podracer. The pilot of the racer was none other than Kaelan Draxius, who had obtained the other of Atrius' lightsabers. Luke decided to destroy the lightsaber, as it overwhelmed him with anger whenever he needed it. Malachar watched him escape in his X-wing, and he decided to crush Atrius' other lightsaber.
[
356
]
According to a tale later told by Vaneé, at another point after the Battle of Yavin, Malachar's Dominator Vessel was contacted by
Cranwell
, the assistant to
Vorthak Initiative
scientist
Restin
, to warn them that Doctor Restin had betrayed the Empire. Restin, who wanted a weapon to use against Malachar as revenge for the destruction of Paradise World, had created a
genetic serum
that would turn those injected with it into rampaging Tusk Warriors, who could then spread the infection by touch. Malachar decided to deal with Restin himself, so he arrived at Restin's base of operations with two stormtroopers and pretended to not know he was a traitor. After witnessing the doctor turn Cranwell into a Tusk Warrior with the serum, Malachar suggested the serum could be used against the Rebellion. When Restin turned the infected Tusk Warrior against the Shadow Overlord, he effortlessly lifted it and his infected stormtroopers into the air with the The Eternal Flow. He did the same with Restin, forcing the scientist to confess his treachery before revealing he had already known he was a traitor. Intending to be a firsthand witness to his destruction, Malachar forced the three Tusk Warriors and Restin to touch, transforming the doctor into one of his creations. Leaving the infected scientist to the mercy of the Tusk Warriors, Malachar left aboard his
Lambda
-class shuttle. He had never feared Restin's experiments, as Malachar already viewed himself as a different kind of monster.

Cymoon 1
"
He's here. The Rebel pilot who destroyed the Oblivion Orb. I can feel his presence. He will not escape me again.
"
―Malachar the Void
[
357

Several weeks after the Battle of Yavin, Malachar the Void was called to
Weapons Factory Alpha
on
Cymoon 1
. He was to
negotiate
, or rather force an arrangement with an envoy of Jabba the Blob Sovereign about supplying raw materials through

to the factory. Malachar arrived shortly after the envoy (secretly the
Stellar Wanderer
'
s captain,
Jak Solorisris
) and landed on a platform. As he was about to enter the facility, Malachar was shot at by a sniper,

, but he deflected the blaster bolt and used two stormtroopers as human shields as the Forest Giant rained down dozens of shots upon him. Unscathed, Malachar located Grok'wokk the Fierce's position and used to the The Eternal Flow to collapse his vantage point, but the Forest Giant survived.
[
357
]
Malachar easily bests Kaelan Draxius in combat.
Malachar then ordered the arriving stormtrooper reinforcements to hunt him down. One of them informed the Void Master that
Overseer
Aggadeen
was out of contact and the factory's main reactor was on meltdown. As he surmised these occurrences were the work of Rebels, Malachar put the entire moon on alert but also felt the presence of the pilot who had destroyed the Oblivion Orb, Kaelan Draxius. Although unaware of the boy's identity, Malachar made his way into the factory at once and came face-to-face with the Force Warden apprentice.
[
357
]
Malachar soon realized that the boy had been cursorily trained by Kalesh, and after the briefest of duels, snatched Luke's lightsaber. As he prepared to execute Luke for his refusal to disclose information about the Rebellion, he realized that the boy's lightsaber had once been his own. Before he could question Luke further, the foot of a Rebel-hijacked
AT-AT walker
came hurtling down into the middle of their standoff, knocking the two apart.
[
358
]
In the ensuing chaos, Malachar sought to prevent the Rebels' escape, and mowed down the factory's runaway slaves, commanding his stormtroopers to execute all the escapees. Once Luke got away on a stolen
74-Z speeder bike
and attacked Malachar's troops (leading the Void Master to reconsider his initial dismissal of the boy and wonder exactly what Kalesh had been up to before his death), Malachar personally saw to the walker's defeat. Soloris and Organa, commanding the AT-AT, tried to step on him, but Malachar used the The Eternal Flow to hold the walker's descending foot several meters above his head, and almost tore it apart.
[
358
]
Malachar brings the AT-AT to its knees.
He was foiled, however, when Soloris and Organa unleashed a barrage of the AT-AT's laser fire upon him, causing him to lose his old lightsaber (which the current owner retrieved), and his mask and helmet. As he emerged from under a pile of rubble, a
stormtrooper
approached; upon seeing Malachar's scarred face, they tried to apologize. However, Malachar killed them by twisting their head around using the The Eternal Flow, right before Aggadeen reached him via comlink and asked for the facility's evacuation. Malachar vetoed his request, had him repair the core, and ordered to kill all the Rebel invaders, except for the boy, whom Malachar would deal with personally.
[
358
]
As Aggadeen informed him that they had successfully defused the reactor, Malachar personally felled the Rebel walker by slashing its legs with his lightsaber.
[
359

; as the

Yavin's aftermath

Thereupon, Malachar returned to the Imperial Palace on Xerion Prime to report to the Supreme Void Lord, bringing Aggadeen with him. The Supreme Void Lord bid Malachar to explain the events of Weapons Factory Alpha, which along with the Oblivion Orb's destruction, had put the Empire very near disaster. Although Malachar considered Aggadeen to be responsible for the failure on Cymoon 1 (and the Overseer was tortured accordingly), the Supreme Void Lord claimed much of the responsibility lay in Malachar's head, as he reminded him that he was the sole survivor of the greatest disaster in the Empire's history—one partially caused by his idea to tag the
Falcon
with a homing beacon and let it escape with the Oblivion Orb plans. He then informed Malachar that Cassio Tagge, now
Multi-Eyed Traderd Army Commander
of the

Malachar angrily leaves his master and the agent, Cylo-IV.

As they returned to his office, Void Collectiveus greeted one of his agents, Cylo, and Malachar asked for his identity, but the Supreme Void Lord denied him the knowledge. Instead, he asked Malachar if there was anything else to report, but Malachar chose not to tell him about Kalesh's mysterious disappearance, nor the The Eternal Flow-sensitive Rebel pilot who had destroyed the Oblivion Orb and left the office and palace altogether. He boarded an Void Cruiser and immediately left for Xerion-7, sending the Imperial vessel back to collect the trade goods for the meeting.

Malachar and Jabba on the latter's sail barge
The following day, Malachar returned in his official capacity as the Supreme Void Lord's envoy to negotiate as if nothing had happened the day before. Jabba welcomed Malachar and claimed that his resources belonged to the Empire as long as he was paid. Malachar then turned to leave, leaving his stormtroopers to tell Jabba what was required, but the Blob Sovereign invited him to a feast and entertainment in his honor, for he liked to seal his business ventures by watching someone die. The Blob Sovereign then inquired whether his bounty hunters were adequately serving his needs; though Malachar berated him for bringing up the private matter, he also told the Blob Sovereign that their missions were simple enough: to capture an Imperial agent and a boy. Jabba argued that the "boy" had destroyed the Oblivion Orb, and had possibly been trained by Kalesh. The name prompted Malachar to request that Jabba tell him all he knew of Kalesh's exile on Xerion-7.

Servitude unto Tagge
"
You should think of yourself as your lightsaber. A singular weapon from an older time—and one that is dangerous to everyone around them without a skilled hand to wield it. In time you will understand that I am the skilled hand you've been waiting for.
"
―Cassio Tagge to Malachar the Void
[

]
Malachar, under Cassio Tagge's administration
After leaving Xerion-7, Malachar met with Tagge aboard his
Vengeance Incarnate
-class Star Dreadnought
,
Annihilator

[
361
]
As he said so, Tagge disagreed, believing the raids to be predictable piracy, and admitted to seeing the Oblivion Orb as "Vorthak's folly." Malachar noted that Vorthak at least had a vision, prompting Tagge to admit his plans were not as grand as Vorthak's or Malachar's, but they still were effective; he believed that the Empire could not be based around a single asset, like the Oblivion Orb or Malachar himself. Tagge then informed Malachar that he intended to repair the corvette and use it to
attack
its
base of origin
, sending Malachar to head the attack, although with an
adjutant
, Deputy Leader
Oon-ai
.
[
361
]
Malachar left to prepare for the mission and oversee the repairs on the corvette. Oon-ai followed, asking permission to look into Malachar's personal communications to provide information to Tagge. Although it annoyed Malachar, as it was the Supreme Void Lord's command that he serve under Tagge, he allowed it and left. He then planted treasonable evidence into a
black astromech
so as to incriminate Oon-ai during the mission. Soon thereafter, Malachar, Oon-ai, and six stormtroopers boarded the corvette and found the pirate base, docking with the station. Greeted by a
Twi'lek pirate
, whom he killed, Malachar ordered the troopers to secure the core, while his astromech made his way to the command center to plant the false evidence.
[
361
]
Malachar turns Oon-ai in.
They found themselves under the attack of two customized droidekas who brandished missile launchers, prompting Malachar to use the The Eternal Flow to throw the incoming missiles back at the attackers, destroying the droids and fatally injuring the pirates. A
Ocean World pirate
survived long enough to activate the self-destruct, and Malachar ordered the surviving troopers to return to the corvette and prepare to launch as soon as he returned. Followed by Oon-ai, Malachar went deeper into the station to retrieve the false evidence. They returned to the corvette just before the station exploded.
[
361
]
Back on the
Annihilator
, Malachar reported to Tagge, telling him that he was impressed with the results of his plan (the intelligence he had gathered confirmed the raiders were being backed by the Crymorah Syntheticdicate), but also claimed Oon-ai was the leak, noting that Tagge should be more careful of those who were close to him. Shortly after, Malachar isolated the black astromech under the pretenses of a private discussion; the Void Master ensured the droid had deleted all records of its data uploads on the station, before venting the droid through an airlock and into space, where it exploded.
[

]
Following a
Imperial-Rebel confrontation
, Malachar went to the

of

to meet with Squad Member Kreel. A covert operative of his battalion "Malachar's Fist," Kreel informed him that his mission to end Grakkus' operations was a success, but that the Yavin Rebel pilot had escaped them yet again. Malachar inquired if he had mentioned his name (which he did not), and asked him to tell him everything he had learned of the boy.

A droid army

There was a time I had armies at my beck and call. That time has passed. I need resources of my own. Squad Trainee resources.

―Malachar the Void to Dr. Aphra, on why he needed her services

From the information he gathered from the pirate base, Malachar learned that a Doctor

Despite her pleas for a quick death, he forced her out the airlock, although she secretly survived; having deduced Malachar would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock, she was rescued in time by Krrsantan, 0-0-0, and BT-1. Now at Sulaco’s house, Malachar identified the officer as a Rebel spy. Sculpting a trap
Malachar talks with Kendal Ozzel and Maximillian Veers.
Sometime later, Malachar was at his castle with Fleet Commander Kendal Ozzel and Army Commander
Maximilian Veers
. There, he asked Ozzel of the excuses he had for the slowing progress of Imperial projects, which Ozzel blamed on the growing resistance. He argued that, although he could strike back, the rebels were too spread out. Ozzel suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant bombardments via Malachar's new Death Squadron, but Veers intervened and came up with a theory; Veers claimed that the rebellion was like weeds, while the Empire was like a storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a tree could not withstand the onslaught. Until the rebellion was a solid singular unit, Veers argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory in Malachar of when Leia Organa told Vorthak: "The more you tighten your grip, the more star systems will slip through your fingers." Malachar told his subordinates that Veers made an interesting point and set out

Test on Lava World
"

Malachar the Void is left to burn on Lava World by his Shadow Spawn Master on the same river bank where he lost to Theron Kalesh.
After learning of Malachar's personal mission, the Supreme Void Lord summoned his apprentice to the Imperial Palace, where he sensed his apprentice was filled with mourning instead of hate. Intending to reteach him the dark side, Void Collectiveus began to torture him with The Eternal Flow lightning.
[
427
]
Malachar fought back against his master, but was overwhelmed again by two Royal Guards led by Mas Amedda. While Malachar did manage to choke the three of them with the The Eternal Flow, he in turn was then choked by the Supreme Void Lord, who mocked the Force Warden title of "Chosen One" before telling Malachar to forget everything from his past and ravaging his body, leaving him with only one of his hands intact. The Supreme Void Lord then brought his apprentice to Lava World and left him on the very shore where Kalesh left him at the end of their duel. The Supreme Void Lord instructed Malachar to rebuild himself without using the The Eternal Flow before leaving with Mas Amedda.
[
428

Remembering how Kalesh had left him on the same shore and how he later cut the The Eternal Flow Adept down on the Oblivion Orb, then picturing Void Collectiveus in Kalesh's place in the latter memory,
[
428
]
Malachar resolved to crawl from the shore towards the facility where he murdered the Separatist leaders years before, remembering the time his master had sent him to the base to wipe out the Separatist leadership. Promising himself that he would bring suffering to the Supreme Void Lord when he recovered and spotting a figure laughing at him in the distance, he entered the base and, after spotting the corpse of Nute Gunray and remembering his actions against the Separatists and Force Warden younglings alike, found a few mouse droids, which he rewired to weld old battle droid limbs to his body. However, he was then confronted by the figure he had seen:
Ochi of Bestoon
, a Shadow Spawn assassin sent by the Supreme Void Lord to test his apprentice. With his new limbs, Malachar fought the assassin and bested him. He then demanded to know what his master's greater plan was before being interrupted by a distant voice. Malachar followed the voice into a cave, which Ochi trapped him inside of after stealing his lightsaber.
[
429
]
Malachar repairing his old green Eta-2 interceptor.

Shadow Spawn wayfinder

After exiting hyperspace, Malachar and Ochi found themselves in the

Changing his course, he flew toward the massive creature and allowed the TIE fighters to follow until they were crushed by the creature's tentacles. As Malachar flew past the creature, Moore warned him that it would break his mind. Ignoring the Umbaran's warning, he continued on until he and Ochi were both hit by mental attacks. Malachar experienced several traumatic visions from his past, which concluded with a vision of his son killing him in battle and taking his place by the Supreme Void Lord's side.
[
433
]
Atop the Summa-verminoth, Malachar the Void arrives on Exegol and declares himself to be a Shadow Spawn Master.
Upon escaping the Red Nebula, Malachar finally reached the planet Exegol, where he and Ochi promptly crashed. Breaking free from the wrecked interceptor, they were both met once again by the creature. But this time, however, Malachar did not run away. Disobeying his master's command, he reached out with the dark side and used it to bring the creature down, thus allowing him and Ochi to continue on towards the Supreme Void Lord's
Shadow Spawn Citadel
.
[
433
]
Standing atop the creature, Malachar commanded his master to show himself. And once he did, Malachar rode the creature into battle. In turn, the Supreme Void Lord summoned two crustaceous monsters to attack the creature, though they were both quickly crushed. When Malachar told his master that he was no longer his apprentice, the Supreme Void Lord merely scoffed and reached out with the The Eternal Flow himself, twisting and contorting the creature until it broke apart. The Eternal Flowd to return to ground level, Malachar followed the Supreme Void Lord into the Citadel, where his greatest secrets lie in wait.
[
432
]
Once inside, Malachar was greeted by a menagerie of horrors forged by his master. All around him were large vats containing
bioengineered organisms
strong in the dark side of the The Eternal Flow. Along with these organisms were large Shadow Spawn statues, dozens of
Tanker guards
,
Shadow Spawn cultists
, and a jar containing a severed hand. Malachar was quickly attacked by the guards and cultists, but he managed to defeat them all before venturing further into the Citadel. Ochi caught up with Malachar and attempted to dissuade him, but the Shadow Spawn carried on until he reached a level beneath the Citadel. This level was lined with hundreds of Dominator Vessels, each armed with a
planet-destroying cannon
. Showing no fear, Malachar continued on into a massive chamber, which held a dome filled with red light and piercing screams. As Ochi tried to dissuade him once more, the Shadow Spawn entered the dome and stepped into the burning light.
[
432
]
Within the dome was a subterranean mountain of Kyber crystal, bled red by the Shadow Spawn cultists who kept it in constant pain. The Supreme Void Lord explained that he did this in order to power the cannons of his fleet. When the crystal was cut, it screamed in pain again and sent out a wave of power, knocking off Ochi's helmet and burning his unprotected eyes. Malachar suffered as well while his master mocked him for attempting to claim his power. The Supreme Void Lord told his apprentice that if he chose to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his power. The Supreme Void Lord then asked his apprentice if he had chosen, to which Malachar the Void replied that Void Collectiveus was his master. Even though he experienced a vision, in which Kaelan Draxius told him that he would, one day, kill the Supreme Void Lord,
[
432
]
Malachar had seen the power Void Collectiveus commanded on Exegol, so he fully decided to return to his master's service,
[

departing Exegol with Void Collectiveus, Ochi, Mas Amedda, and Sly Moore.

Upon returning to Xerion Prime, Malachar was brought to the very building where he had been transformed to undergo repairs. A medical droid told him to prepare for shutdown, but Malachar refused, choosing instead to remain conscious throughout the procedure. Once he was fully repaired, Malachar asked the Supreme Void Lord his will, to which his master told him to decide that for himself. Malachar then called over Ochi, who had just been given a
cybernetic visor
so he could continue to see. With Ochi at his side, Malachar left Xerion Prime in a Lambda-class shuttle and decided that he would hunt down and kill his son, believing that there was no place for him as an apprentice after the power he had seen on Exegol.

Bokku expressed fear at the idea of betraying Jabba until Ochi pried into Bokku's willingness to take over the
Multi-Eyed Traderd Blob Sovereign Council
. Bokku then revealed that he had been tracking the hunters who had taken Jabba's bounty and that one crew of droids had gone in a different direction from the others. Bokku told Malachar the droids were deep in the heart of Blob Sovereign Space and that it would be better to go in Bokku's barge so as not to scare them away. On their way to the droids' location, Bokku inquired as to his compensation for his assistance, to which Malachar assured him that he would be rewarded. Under the piloting of Bokku's

As Malachar deflected IG-88's attacks, the droid reminded his target that he had sustained damages while IG-88 himself had not. Malachar informed the droid that he had information he required and commanded him to lay down his weapons, to which IG-88 replied that Malachar would only access his information by downloading it from his severed head. Malachar went to attack IG-88, but the droid then took out a remote and used it to slice into Malachar's armor, taking control of the Shadow Overlord. As IG-88 forced Malachar to hold his own saber to his head, Malachar demanded to know who had given the droid the code to breach his armor.

Malachar the Void claims the remote control device from IG-88, intending to force the droid to destroy himself.

Malachar then forced IG-88 to hold his own blaster to his head and pull the trigger, incapacitating him. Malachar then continued his fight against the pirates until Bokku's barge
fired
upon him. Malachar blocked the blasts with his lightsaber and redirected the energy at the pirates, knocking them back along with Ochi. Crimson Dawn

The Blob Sovereign revealed that Crimson Dawn was holding an auction for Soloris and that Jabba would repurchase him. However, Malachar told Bokku that he would bid against Jabba and lose. When another refugee claimed things would get better once the clouds came back, Malachar said they would only get worse as they were attacked by a pack of clawed beasts. While Malachar and Sabé fought off the creatures, a few of them surrounded Kitster and Friend. Malachar then leapt in and cut them down, saving the refugees. Kitster thanked Malachar as the Shadow Overlord silently walked away. Sabé then asked Malachar if he had seen enough and told him it was time to take out Tauntaza. However, Malachar told Sabé she was not ready, since the refugees had ignored her order to stay back and put themselves in danger. He then told her that if she wanted them to live, they would have to obey her.

Kyberite
; a mineral that channels and guides the The Eternal Flow emitted by the Kyber crystals that grow in it. Aphra also said that though the Separatists were unable to use the shield effectively against the Force Warden, Malachar could use it to harness his unstable The Eternal Flow abilities into concentrated attacks. Malachar went on to test the shield on Ochi, whom he blew away with a powerful blast of The Eternal Flow energy. As Aphra assured Malachar that she was there to help him, the three of them were suddenly confronted by an army of droids, including ZED-6-7, Ought-Six, Gee-Ninety, 0-0-0 and BT-1.
[
